uyirmey variyum eḻuttu muṟiyum tamiḻukkē ciṟappākum. intiya moḻikaṭkellām ivi piṟkālattē tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟi yēṟpaṭṭaṉa. arapi,  hīpru mutaliya cēmiyak (Semitic) kavimoḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum, camaṟkiruta moḻinta āriyak kavimoḻikaḷ ellā vaṟṟiṟkum iviyilli. eḻuttukaḷ iṅkilīṣiṟ pōla uyirum meyyum vēṟu vēṟu aṭuttaṭut teḻutappaṭuvatuṭaṉ, arivariyil uyirum meyyum muṟiyiṉṟi mayaṅkik kiṭakkum. āriyamum cēmiyamu mākiya mēli moḻikaṭkellām uyirmey vaṭivum, eḻuttu muṟiyum illātirukkavum, camaṟkirutattiṟku maṭṭum avi eṅṅaṉa miruntirut tal kūṭum? camaṟkirutattiṟku avi iruntaṉaveṉiṉ, ataṉ iṉamākiya cerumaṉ, ṭaccu, kirēkku, ilattīṉ mutaliya moḻikaṭkum, avaṟṟi aṭuttu vaḻaṅkum cēmiya moḻikaṭkum ēṉ aviyilli?  vaṭamoḻi āriyar intiyāviṟku vantatu ki.mu. 3000. avar varukikku muṉṉarē tamiḻil eḻuttiruntatu . av veḻuttukaḷ uyirmey  nīṅkalāka 31. 
olimuṟiyiṉum coṉmuṟiyiṉum tamiḻukku aṭuttuḷḷa kirēkka, ilattīṉ moḻikaḷil muṟiyē eḻuttukaḷ 24, 25;  vaṭamoḻiyil 48. ilattīṉ eḻuttukaḷ 24 eṉṟum, 23 eṉṟum kaṇakkiṭuvatumuṇṭu. tamiḻilillāta cila ceyaṟki yolikaḷ ilattīṉ, kirēkku    moḻikaḷi luḷa. āṉāl, avaṟṟiṟ palavaṟṟiṟkut taṉi varikaḷ illi. B,G, mutaliya toṉippolikaṭkut taṉi variyuḷa. bh, gh  mutaliya mūccoli cērnta toṉippolikaṭkut taṉivari yilli. camaṟkirutattil avaṟṟiṟ kellām taṉivari yuṇṭu. atōṭu ña, ṇa mutaliya tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷum cērkkappaṭṭuḷa. mēli moḻikaṭkum tamiḻukkumuḷḷa eḻuttukaḷiṟ perumpālaṉavaṟṟiṟkut taṉivari yamittu vaṭamoḻi  yeḻuttukaḷip perukkik kāṭṭiṉar vaṭamoḻivāṇar. ka,  ca, ṭa, ta,  pa eṉṟu tamiḻmuṟiyil valliṉa meyyeḻuttukaḷiruppatum, k, kh, g, gh eṉa ovvoru valliṉa meyyum toṉippatum, toṉiyātatum, mūccoli cērntatum cērātatumāka nannāṉkāka  uṟaḻappaṭṭiruppatum, camaṟkirutattiṉ ceyaṟkiyāṉa eḻuttup perukkik kāṭṭum.
camaṟkirutam, kirēkka ilattīṉ moḻikaḷiṉ tiripu allatu vaḷarcci yeṉpataṟkup pala ātāraṅkaḷuḷa. kirēkka ilattīṉ moḻikaḷ āriya moḻikaḷil mikap paḻimiyāṉavi. kirēkka ilattīṉ eḻuttukaḷiṉum camaṟkiruta eḻuttukaḷ mikuti. kirēkka ilattīṉiluḷḷa  pala coṟkaḷ camaṟkirutattil mikat tirintuḷḷaṉa. camaṟkirutam eṉṉum peyarukkē naṉṟākac ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatu poruḷ. ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṉum eḻuttup perukkamum ceyaṟkiyolip perukka muḷḷatu camaṟkirutamoṉṟē. ataṉāṉēyē atu vaḻakkaṟ ṟoḻintatu.
vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷi 48 ākap perukki yiruppiṉum, oli muṟippaṭi pārppiṉ, avaṟṟiṟ puṇarolikaḷ allatu kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ palavākum.
`ru', `lu' eṉpavi kuṟṟiyalukaram cērnta rakara lakaraṅkaḷākum. `rū' eṉpatu ru eṉpataṉ nīṭṭam.  `am' eṉpatu am eṉṉum aci;  akara uyirum makarameyyum cērntatu. ivi uyirmeykaḷum aciyumāka viruntum uyireḻuttukaḷiṟ  cērkkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. e, o eṉṟa uyirkaḷ vaṭamoḻikkillātatu oru peruṅ kuṟiyākum.
meyyeḻuttukaḷil ai varukkattil h cērnta eḻuttukaḷellām iraṭṭi yeḻuttukaḷākum. cantiyakṣaraṅkaḷ cila taṉi  yeḻuttukaḷpōṟ kāṭṭappaṭum. itaṉāl vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttup perukkiṉāl oru ciṟappu milli yeṉka.
vaṭamoḻi āriyar intiyāviṟku vanta piṉṉarē uyirmey variyum eḻuttumuṟiyum vaṭamoḻikku ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. vaṭamoḻi varikaḷ teṉmoḻi varikaḷiṉ tiripē eṉpatu irumoḻi varikaḷiyum oppu nōkkuvārkku iṉitu pulaṉām. vaṭamoḻi yarivari yēṟpaṭṭa piṉ ati yoṭṭi ataṉ kiḷimoḻikaṭkum piṉpu tamiḻk kiḷimoḻikaṭkum arivarikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl perciyaṉ arapi varikaḷik koṇṭa intustāṉi yoḻinta ēṉi yintiya moḻikaṭkellām uyirmey variyum eḻuttumuṟiyum ēṟpaṭṭuḷa.
ātiyil ēṉiya moḻikaṭkellā millātu uyirmey varikaḷ tamiḻuk kiruntatiṉālēyē, ataṉ arivari nīṇṭu neṭuṅkaṇak keṉappaṭṭatu. ēṟkeṉavē neṭuṅkaṇakkā yirukkum arivariyi (centamiḻp puravalar umāmakēcuvaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ kūṟiyapaṭi) iṉṉum neṭuṅkaṇakkākka niṉippatu eṉṉē pētimi!
iṉi, oru moḻikku nirampiṉa arivari yiruppiṉum, piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷi atil moḻipeyarkkap pōtiya coṟkaḷuḷavā  eṉṟu pārttal vēṇṭum. ilaveṉiṉ piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyum piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷiyum atiṟ putitāyc cērkka iṭamuṇṭu. tamiḻil ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum veḷiyiṭattakka eṇṇiṟanta coṟkaḷuḷḷaṉa. ātaliṉ piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyēṉum eḻuttukaḷiyēṉum putitāyc cērkka eṭṭuṇiyum iṭamiṉṟu. poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappup peyar (Proper Name) kaḷē eḻuttup bga®¥ã‰F«(Transliteration)  taṟpavattiṟkum iṭamākum. itaṟkup piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷ  vēṇṭuvavalla. tamiḻil iyaṉṟatuṇi eḻutal takum. ētēṉuṅ kuṟiyiruppiṉ atu ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvākum. itaṟkeṉṟē tamiḻiṟ putteḻuttukaḷic cērppiṉ tamiḻiṉ tūymiyum iyalpum eḷimiyum iṉimiyum keṭum. aṅṅaṉam tamiḻik keṭuttu vaḻaṅkuvatiṉum, ati viṭṭuviṭṭu ayaṉ moḻiyi vaḻaṅkuvatē cālac ciṟantatām. ataṉāṟ putteḻuttukaḷit tamiḻiṟ pukuttavēṇṭum eṉṉum poruntāk kūṟṟip puṟattē viṭukka.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' naḷi 1934
14
tamiḻ eḻuttu māṟṟam
viṭutalipeṟṟa intiyāvil, tamiḻnāṭu pōmpōkkum  tamiḻppulavar pōmpōkkum tamiḻākkattiṟ kētuvāyt tōṉṟavilli. tamiḻatikāram mellamellat tamiḻaṟiyāta perumāḷar kikkum tamiḻp pakivar kikkum naḻuvikkoṇṭirukkiṉṟatu. ūruk kiḷitta vaṉ piḷḷiyār kōyi lāṇṭi eṉpatupōl, tamiḻum tamiḻp pulavarum evarālum eṅṅaṉamum māṟṟappaṭaṟ kuriyarāyiṉar. tamiḻip pala tuṟiyilum māṟṟuvataṟkē ellōrum muḻuvurimi eytiyuḷḷaṉar.
tamiḻ vaḷarppiṟku oru potukkaḻakam illāmi
paṇṭikkālattil pāṇṭināṭṭut tamiḻkkaḻakam oṉṟē  tamiḻakam muḻumikkum potuvāyiruntu, kula mata vēṟupāṭaṟṟut tamiḻākkam oṉṟiyē kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭu, mutunūlārāycciyum putunūlākkamu mākiya iruvakit toṇṭāṟṟi, tollāciriyar nallāṇi vaḻiyē evvakiyilum eḷḷaḷavum tamiḻmarapu keṭātu, tamiḻi vaḷarttu vantatu. tamiḻakattiluḷḷa  perumpulavar ellārum atil cērntiruntaṉar. paricaḷippu muṟiyil pāṇṭiyar avarip pōṟṟi vantaṉar. āriyam tamiḻakattu vērūṉṟumvariyum, pāṇṭiyar toṉṟu toṭṭuk  kaḻakam niṟuvit tamiḻi vaḷarttuvantatākat terikiṉṟatu. kaṭalkōḷkaḷāl nērnta iṭavēṟupāṭṭāl kaḻakam mūṉṟeṉappaṭṭatē yaṉṟi, ataṉ kulivāl nērnta iṭiyīṭṭālaṉṟu. moḻiyum ilakkiyamum paṟṟiya muḻu atikāramum kaḻakattiṟkē iruntatu.
taṟkālamō, atikāramaṟṟa muṟiyil, tamiḻi vaḷarttaṟkō taḷarttaṟkō, maturit tamiḻccaṅkam eṉṟum, karantit tamiḻccaṅkam eṉṟum, nellit tamiḻccaṅkam eṉṟum, ceṉṉit tamiḻccaṅkam eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum, paṟpala caṅkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi vevvēṟu koḷkiyuṅ kōṭpāṭuṅ koṇṭu, ovvāta muṟiyil paṟpala viṉikaḷum vēlikaḷum ceytuvarukiṉṟaṉa. ivi pōtāveṉṟu iṉṟu tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakam eṉṟum oṉṟu tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu. ivaṟṟiṉ toḻilkaḷi nōkkuṅkāl, kula mata kaṭcic cārpāṉa tamiḻc caṅkaṅkaḷāyt tōṉṟu kiṉṟaṉavē yoḻiya, ellārkkum potuvāṉa tamiḻccaṅkaṅkaḷāyt tōṉṟavilli. avi aṅṅaṉamiruppiṉ, oṉṟāyirukkum; allatu iṇikkappaṭṭirukkum.
iṉi, atikāra muṟiyil, araciyalār pāṭapputtakak kuḻuvum, palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻppāṭak kuḻuvum uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟāl oppampeṟum puttakaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟil eḻuttup piḻi,   coṟpiḻi, ilakkaṇappiḻi iruppiṉum irukkum.
iṅṅaṉam, tamiḻppulavar kuḻāṅkaḷ palvēṟupaṭṭuk kiṭappatālum, tamiḻppulamiyum tamiḻppaṟṟum illātārum pala tamiḻccaṅka vuṟuppiṉarāyiruppatālum, cila tamiḻp perumpulavar oru caṅkattilum cērātiruppatālum, kuṟṟampaṭa  nūliyaṟṟuvārik kuṭṭutaṟkum veṭṭu taṟkum oruvarumiṉmiyālum; varivaṭivi māṟṟuvārum, puṇarcciyit tirippārum, ceyyuḷkaḷik kaḷivārum, ayaleḻuttiyum colli yum pukuttuvārum, pāṭavēṟupāṭu ceyvārum, taṉcolli ayaṟ collākak kāṭṭuvārum, mutaṉūli vaḻinūlākkuvārum, collukkum ceyyuṭkum ovvāta  poruḷ kūṟuvārumāy; palkuḻuvum pāḻceyum uṭpakiyum kolkuṟumpum tamiḻukkut tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam  āḷāḷukkōr ampalam paṇṇuvatāl, tamiḻi marapuneṟi vaḻātu vaḷarttaṟkilli. cila tāṉṟōṉṟit tampirāṉmār tamakkuc caṟṟu araciyalatikāram vāyttavuṭaṉ, nātiyaṟṟa kōvilukku nāṉtāṉaṭā pūcāri eṉṟu cila kuḻukkaḷik kūṭṭic cila nitikaḷiyum tiraṭṭit tamakkup pērum pukaḻum varuvatiyē payaṉākakkoṇṭu, pala tamiḻt taḷarcci vēlikaḷic ceytuvarukiṉṟaṉar.
tirukkuṟaḷip puṟakkaṇippavarum tamiḻppulavarip paḻippavarum kūṭat tamiḻttalivarāyk kiḷarnteḻuvatu  vintiyiṉum vintiyē!
iruvaki yatikāram
m¿tâfhu«(Literary Authority), M£áaâfhu«(Administrative   Authority) eṉa atikāram iruvakittu. ivaṟṟuḷ, aṟivatikāram kalviyāl tāṉāy varuvatu; nilittatu: āṭciyatikāram piṟar amarttattāl allatu tērtalāl varuvatu; niliyaṟṟatu. aṟivatikāri āṭcivakiyil āṭciyatikārikku eṅṅaṉam aṭaṅkutal vēṇṭumō, aṅṅaṉamē āṭciyatikāriyum aṟivuvakiyil aṟivatikārikku aṭaṅkutalvēṇṭum. itu eṉṟum cellum  ulakap potuviti. aṟivatikārikaḷellām āṭciyatikārikaḷāka irukka muṭiyum; āyiṉ āṭciyatikārikaḷellām aṟivatikārikaḷāka irukka muṭiyātu.
āṅkilat tamiḻc ceytittāḷāciriyar ēṟṟattāḻvu
mēlnāṭṭilum kīḻnāṭṭilum āṅkilac ceytittāḷāciriya rellārum ciṟanta ilakkiya aṟiñar; allatu kuṟinta pakkam  nalla moḻiyaṟiñar. tamiḻc ceytittāḷāciriyarō perumpālum ilakkaṇa vilakkiyap payiṟciyillātavar; pulavar tērvu tēṟātavar. āyiṉum, pulavarvaḻit tām niṟkātu tamvaḻip  pulavar niṟka virumpukiṉṟaṉar.
tūya tamiḻccoṟka ḷiruppavum, avaṟṟukkup patilākap piṟa coṟkaḷi yāṇṭu kalavinaṭiyil eḻuti varuvatē, tamiḻc  ceytit tāḷāciriyar vaḻakkamā yiruntuvarukiṉṟatu. ilakkiya aṟivum moḻip paṟṟum illātavar tamiḻukku eṅṅaṉam atikārikaḷāka muṭiyum?
ōriru pulavarkkut tamiḻeḻuttu māṟṟa atikāramiṉmi
iṉi, perum paṭṭamum perum pataviyum peruñ campaḷamum peṟṟiruttal kāraṇamāka, iraṇṭoru pulavar maṭṭum tamiḻukku muḻu atikārikaḷākiviṭār. kaliyāṇacuntaraṉār, paṇṭitamaṇiyār, pāratiyār mutaliya perumpulavariyum piṟamoḻi yaṟiñariyum kēṭṭē, tamiḻ eḻuttil māṟṟañceytal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻeḻuttu māṟṟum muḻuvatikāramum tamakkuṇṭeṉṟu ōriru pulavar tarukkuvarēl, avar palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyil tamiḻ vaṭamoḻi vaḻittākak kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppati oṭṭiyō veṭṭiyō pēcit tam karutti nāṭṭit tam atikārattik kāṭṭuka.
viṉiyiṉ iyalpiyum piṉviḷiviyuma ciṟitum karutātu, oru  kuḻuvil iṭam peṟṟatē pōtumeṉṟu makiḻvatu, pulamakkaḷum potu makkaḷumākiya irucār tamiḻarkkum ikkālattiyalpā yuḷḷatu.
tamiḻeḻuttiṉ cīrmiyum piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷiṉ kuṟipāṭum
itupōtu vaḻakkiluḷḷa tamiḻ arivariyip piṟamoḻi  yarivarikaḷōṭu oppunōkkiṉāltāṉ, muṉṉatiṉ cīrmi viḷaṅkum.
vaṭamoḻi yarivariyil, gh, bh; ṅa, ṭa; bh, m ākiya iv vīreḻuttukaḷ ciṟitē tammuḷ vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa. kh eṉṉum eḻuttum sva eṉṉum kūṭṭeḻuttum, rava pōlum tōṉṟum. muṉṉum piṉṉum kīḻum cērtteḻutum kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ (r«í¡jhBu«) 160-kku mēluṇṭu. itaṉāl mika nuṇukkiyum varinerukkiyum eḻutamuṭiyātu. kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ palavaṟṟiṟkut taṉi accurukkaḷ (types) vēṇṭum. uyirmey varikaḷ (33 x 12) 462.
vaṭamoḻi yarivariyiyē koṇṭa inti yeḻuttukaḷil ik kuṟipāṭṭōṭu iṭi kaṭi varum akara vūmiyeḻuttukaḷum (Silent Letters) uṇṭu. uyirmey varikaḷ (33 x 12) 396.
teluṅkarivariyil, o, bh; pa, va; na, sa ākiya ivvīreḻuttukaḷ tammuḷ mikacciṟitē vēṟupaṭṭu mayakkattiṟkiṭamāvaṉa. muṉṉum piṉṉuṅ kīḻum cērtteḻutuṅ kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ iṭatti  aṭippaṉa, mo, mō, yi, yī, yo, yō, ho, hō ākiya uyirmeyyeḻuttukaḷ ēṉiya vaṟṟiṉum vēṟupaṭṭa  uyirkkuṟi yuṭiyaṉa. uyirmey varikaḷ (35 x 35) 455. iviyallātu, nakārappollu eṉṉum oru taṉi ṉakaramey variyum, paṇṭira allatu cakaṭṭirēppa eṉṉum ṟakarameyyum, oru meykku allatu uyirmeykkuppiṉ eḻuti muṉ palukkappeṟum velappala kilaka eṉṉum rakaravariyum uḷḷaṉa.
kaṉṉaṭa arivari teluṅkarivariyip piṉpaṟṟiyatātaliṉ, atilum ittakiya kuṟipāṭuṇṭu. kaṉṉaṭa uyirmeyvarikaḷ (34 x 14) 476.
maliyāḷa arivariyil, (kuṟṟiyalukara) lu, ña eṉṉum īreḻuttum  kh, ch, p, v ākiya nāleḻuttum, mayakkattiṟkiṭamāvaṉa. ku, kū eṉṉum īreḻuttum nuṇukkeḻuttil kūrṅkaṇṇarukkaṉṟi vēṟupāṭu teriyā. ukara ūkāramēṟṟa uyirmeykaḷ ōriyal (uniform) vaṭivu koṇṭaṉa valla. muṉṉum piṉṉum kīḻum mēlum cērtteḻutum kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ         300-kku mēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟukkēṉum taṉi accurukkaḷ vēṇṭum. ch, b, y, v ākiya  nāleḻuttum iraṭṭikkum kūṭṭeḻuttu kaḷil kīḻeḻuttu oṉṟākavē irukkum. uyirmey varikaḷ (36 x 16) 576.
tamiḻilō ovvōr eḻuttum eḷitāy vēṟupaṭuttaṟiyakkūṭiya taṉi vaṭivuḷḷatu. meykaḷ patiṉeṭṭē. itaṉālum, kūṭṭeḻuttiṉmi yālum tamiḻeḻuttut toki piṟa intiya moḻiyeḻuttut toki yiṉum ettuṇiyō kuṟikiṉṟatu. uyirmeyvarikaḷ (18 x 12) 216. āytam oṉṟu. āka, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ mottam 247tām.
cila uyirmeyvarikaḷ tamiḻil ōriyal vaṭivu koṇṭaṉavalla veṉiṉ, ik kuṟipāṭu teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa maliyāḷattilum uḷḷatē! ataṟkeṉ ceyvatu?
urutuviṟkuriya pāracīka-arapi yarivariyil uyirmey varikaḷ illāviṭiṉum, palaveḻuttukaḷ orē vaṭivukoṇṭu mēṟkīḻp puḷḷittoki vēṟupāṭṭālēyē vēṟupaṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  perum pālum, ovvōreḻuttiṟkum colliṉ mutaliṭi kaṭikkuriya mūvēṟu vaṭivuṇṭu. yakaravari ikarattiyum, vakaravari ukarattiyum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
nuṇukkiyum nerukkiyum aḻakākavum teḷivākavum maṇipōl eḻutappeṟum eḻuttukaḷuḷ talimiyāṉatu rōma (Roman)  eḻuttu. ataṟkaṭuttatu tamiḻē. tamiḻ eḻuttu vaṭivi māṟṟiṉāl, pala iṉṉalum aḻakiḻappum nērum.
piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷil ettuṇiyō kuṟipāṭirukkumpōtu, avaṟṟiyellām kavaṉikkāmal  tamiḻeḻutti maṭṭum māṟṟuvatu eṟṟukku? mēlum, oru allatu cila ceytittāḷil eḻutti māṟṟa virumpiṉ, atupaṟṟip pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷilum ilakkiyattilum ēṉ māṟṟavēṇṭum? ēṟkeṉavē tamiḻeḻuttup palamuṟi takunta muṟiyil cīrtiruttap peṟṟē iṟṟi vaṭiveytiyuḷḷateṉpati, tamiḻ eḻuttu māṟṟak kōṭpāṭṭiṉar aṟivārā? avar virumpuvatu cīrtiruttamā? allatu (cīrkēṭāṉa) māṟṟamā?
tamiḻeḻuttil ceyyavēṇṭiya tiruttam
tamiḻeḻuttil ippōtu ceyyavēṇṭiya tiruttam iraṇṭē. oṉṟu īyi nīkki `     ' yi vittukkoḷvatu; iṉṉoṉṟu   aukāra uyirilum ataṉiyēṟṟa uyirmeykaḷilum ḷa variyic caṟṟuc  ciṟitākkuvatu.       varitāṉ mutalāvatu iruntatu. piṉpu iṭikkālattil atu vilakkappaṭṭuk kiranta variyākiya ī  pukuttappaṭṭatu. aukāra variyiluḷḷa ḷa variyum ū variyiṟpōl mutalāvatu ciṟitākavē yiruntu piṟkālattil tavaṟākap peritāka eḻutappaṭṭatu.
tamiḻppulavar ceyta tavaṟu
in nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻuttuppaṟṟit tamiḻukku iruvakiyil ūṟu nērntapōtu tamiḻppulavar taṭuttilar. oru moḻiyil pala  karuttukaṭkum collirukkumpōtu, avaṟṟukkup patilākap piṟamoḻic  coṟkaḷi yum, eḻuttukaḷiyum vēṇṭātu pukuttuvatu, oru moḻiyik keṭukkum vaḻiyākum.
tamiḻil orē yuyirkoṇṭa uyirmey varikaḷellām ōriyal vaṭivukoṇṭiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu tiruntiya karuttaṉṟu.  eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka aikāra uyirmeykaḷi eṭuttukkoḷḷiṉ,  avi ōriyal vaṭivukoṇṭirāmikkut takunta kāraṇamuṇṭu.        eṉpataṟkup patilāka ṉi eṉṟu eḻutiṉ, kūṭṭeḻuttāyeḻutuṅ kiyeḻuttil atu iru ṉakarampōl tōṉṟum. im mayakkatti nīkkutaṟkē, aikāra vuyirkkuṟi, pala cuḻikaḷum vaḷivukaḷuṅ koṇṭa eḻuttukaṭkellām mēliṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉamē āyntu nōkkiṉ, ōriyal vaṭivu peṟāta piṟa uyirmey varikaṭkum kāraṇam tōṉṟum. accuvaṭivu oṉṟiyē kavaṉittuk kiyeḻuttu vaṭivi nōkkātavarkkē, tamiḻ uyirmey varikaḷ oḻuṅkillātaṉavākat tōṉṟalām. kiyeḻuttum mukkiyam; āṅkilattil ataṉiyum accu vaṭivil amittiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
' eṉṉum paḻiya aikāra vuyirmeyk kuṟiyi `' eṉṟu  iṇittum, ku ṅu cu ñu ṭu ṇu mutaliya uyirmey varikaḷi orē   muṟiyil variyum oṟṟik kuṟiyākavum eḻutuvatellām  eḷimiyum teḷivum maṭṭumaṉṟu, kālac cikkaṉamum paṟṟiyatākum. ātalāl, evvakiyilum (mēṟkāṭṭiya iru varikaḷiyaṉṟi) māṟṟam ceyyat tēviyilli. putiya eḻuttumāṟṟam ceytittāḷkaṭku iṉṟiyamiyāta tāyiṉ, avimaṭṭum ati āḷaṭṭum. ēṉiyarkku vēṇṭā.
iṉṟu ceyya vēṇṭiyatu
putiya eḻuttu māṟṟam pukuttappaṭiṉ, palkalikkaḻakap pāṭap  puttakak kuḻuviyum, taṉippaṭṭavarkkum potumakkaṭkumuriya ellā nūlniliyaṅkaḷiyum, tamiḻnūl veḷiyīṭṭāḷariyum, tamiḻc caṅkaṅ kaḷiyum, palavaki āvaṇaṅkaḷiyum, māṇavariyum, tākkip pala iṭarppāṭṭi viḷivikkum. ātalāl, itip pukuttātavāṟu araciya lāri vēṇṭikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
yāḻppāṇat tamiḻar putiya eḻuttumāṟṟatti oppukkoḷḷa villi yeṉṟum, paḻiya eḻuttiyē kaṭippiṭikkat tīrmāṉittuḷḷa ṉar eṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻccoṟkaḷip pōṉṟē tamiḻ eḻuttiyum tūymiyākap pēṇat tuṇinta yāḻppāṇam taḻit tōṅka!
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' navampar 1951
15
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku
(kavaṉippu: nāṉ kaṭanta ari nūṟṟāṇṭākac ceytu vanta collārāycci moḻiyārāycciyiṉ payaṉākak kaṇṭa uṇmikaḷiyum koṇṭa muṭipukaḷiyum, nūlāka uruvākkumuṉ pala kaṭṭurit toṭarkaḷāka veḷiyiṭak karutic `centamiḻc celvi'kku viṭuttu vantēṉ. avaṟṟuḷ iṟutiyāka veḷivanta toṭar, ``tamiḻiṉ talimiyi nāṭṭum taṉic coṟkaḷ'' eṉpatu. atu aimpatu kaṭṭuri koṇṭatu. avaṟṟuḷ patiṉāṉku maṭṭum veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.
iṉṟu tiṭumeṉa etirpārāta oru nilimi nērntu, `ai au' `ay av' tāṉā? eṉṟum, tamiḻukku eḻuttu māṟṟam iṉṟiyamiyātatā veṉṟum, palar nērilum eḻuttu vāyilākavum mēṉmēlum viṉavi varuvatāl, avaraṉivarkkum viṭiyiṟukku mukamāka, (1) tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku, (2) `ai au' `ay av' tāṉā? (3) ekara okara iyaṟki, (4) uyirmeyk kuṟikaḷiṉ ōriyaliṉmi, (5) aṟiyāmi yakaṟṟal. (6) pakuttaṟiviṉ payaṉ eṉṉum aṟu talippuk kaṭṭurikaḷi iṉṟu ovvoṉṟākac celviyil veḷiyiṭat tuṇintēṉ.
tamiḻnā ṭeṅkaṇumuḷḷa pulavar kallūri āciriyarum māṇavarum ūṉṟik kavaṉikka.
tamiḻp paṟṟāḷar āyirak kaṇakkāka acciṭṭup paṭikkat terinta tamiḻar aṉivarkkum āṅkāṅku vaḻaṅkuka.
`centamiḻc celvi' vāṅkāta kalvi niliyaṅkaḷilum nūlakaṅkaḷi lum, āṇṭuk kiyoppaṅ kaṭṭi oru paṭiyēṉum oḻuṅkāka varuvikku māṟu, poṟuppuḷḷa tamiḻp pulavarum tamiḻp paṟṟāḷarum muyaṟci mēṟkoḷka.)
tamiḻ ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻi. iv vuṇmiyi muṉṉamē oru paḻamperum pulavar kaṇṭu,
``ōṅka liṭivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉiyatu
taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ''
eṉṟu teḷḷat teḷivākap pāṭivittirukkiṉṟār. itu uṇmi naviṟciyē yaṉṟi uyarvu naviṟciyaṉṟu.
kumarimuṉikkut teṟkē maṭakācukkart tīvuvari paraviyirunta paḻampāṇṭi nāṭāṉa kumarikkaṇṭam muḻutum mūvēṟu kaṭalkōḷkaḷāl muḻukip pōṉamiyālum, mutaliru kaḻakap paltuṟiyilakkiya vilakkaṇap pulaviya maṉittum iyaṟkiyāluñ ceyaṟkiyālum aḻiyuṇṭami yālum, tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyi āḻnta moḻiyārāycciyiṉṟi aṟiya viyalāvāṟu, moḻiyoḻinta piṟa cāṉṟukaḷellām muṟṟum iṟantu paṭṭaṉa eṉpati, iṟṟit tamiḻp pulavar mutaṟkaṇ aṟital vēṇṭum. ākavē, tolporuṭ kali (Archeology), āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa paḻantamiḻaka varalāṟṟiṟkup payaṉpaṭā teṉpatit tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟut tuṟit talivarum kalveṭṭut tuṟit talivarum tol poruḷārāyccit tuṟit talivarum aṟital vēṇṭum.
tamiḻ teṉṉāṭṭu moḻiyā naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkari moḻiyā eṉṟum, kumarinila moḻiyā iṟṟinila moḻiyā eṉṟum, tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkut tāyā uṭaṉpiṟappā eṉṟum, āriya moḻik kuṭumpattiṟku mūlamāka vuḷḷatā illatā eṉṟum, tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum māntaṉ piṟantakamum oṉṟā vēṟā eṉṟum, moḻi nūl varalāṟu māntaṉūl ākiya muttuṟiyilum, naṭuniliyuṭaṉum poṟumiyuṭaṉum nīṇṭakāla ārāycci ceytālaṉṟi, tamiḻiṉ toṉmi muṉmi tāymi talimi mutaliya taṉmikaḷi muṟṟum aṟiyamuṭiyātu.
tolkāppiyam, mutaliru kaḻakamum irunta paḻam pāṇṭināṭu muḻutum muḻukiya piṉpum, āriyap pūcāriyar teṉṉāṭu vantu āriyac coṟkaḷum karuttukaḷum tamiḻilakkiyattiṟ pukat toṭaṅkiya piṉpum, viki maturiyum kaṭikkaḻakamum tōṉṟumuṉ ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, pāṇṭiyaṉ vayappaṭṭa teṉ cēranāṭṭil tōṉṟiya paṉmāṇ cārpiṟ cārpāṉa tokuppu nūlātaliṉ, ataṉiyē mutaṉūlākak koṇṭu atu tōṉṟiya kālamē tamiḻilakkiyap poṟkālameṉṟu kūṟuvatu, oru peruṅkōviliṉ tiruvuṇṇāḻikiyiyum iṭi maṇṭapattiyum mukamaṇṭapattiyum pārātu, koṭikkampattaṭiyiluḷḷa ciṟṟuruvattiyē kōyiṟpaṭimiyākak karutit toḻuvatu pōṉṟatē.
``centami ḻiyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu
muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟippaṭa eṇṇip
pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval''
eṉṟu paṉampāraṉār kūṟiyatum;
``eḻutteṉappaṭupa.................K¥g~ teṉpa''
eṉṟu eḻuttatikārat toṭakkattilum,
``uyartiṇi yeṉmaṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē''
eṉṟu collatikārat toṭakkattilum,
``kikkiḷi mutalā............vGâiz yeṉpa''
``akattiṇi maruṅkiṉ ariltapa vuṇarntōr
................
tiṟappaṭak kiḷappiṉ''
``nallicip pulavar ceyyu ḷuṟuppeṉa
vallitiṟ kūṟi vakutturit taṉarē.''
``māṟṟaruñ ciṟappiṉ marapiyal kiḷappiṉ''
eṉṟu muṟiyē poruḷatikāra akattiṇiyiyal puṟattiṇiyiyal ceyyuḷiyal marapiyal toṭakkattilum, kūṟiyatōṭu, nūl neṭukalum ``eṉmaṉār pulavar'', ``eṉa moḻipa'', ``variyār'', ``eṉpa uṇarumōrē'', ``eṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē'', ``eṉmaṉār pulami yōrē'', ``eṉamoḻipa uṇarntici ṉōrē'', ``kiḷappa'', ``eṉṟici ṉōrē'' eṉṟum piṟavāṟum aṭikkaṭi iṭiyiṭi tolkāppiyar kūṟic celvatum; tolkāppiyam tokuppu nūlēyaṉṟip putiya vakuppu nūlaṉṟu eṉpatit teḷivākak kāṭṭum. 
mēlum, tolkāppiyam naṉṉūlum aṣṭātyāyī eṉṉum pāṇiṉīyamum pōla eḻuttuñ collum maṭṭuṅ kūṟātu, tamiḻukkē ciṟappākavuḷḷa yāppaṇi yuṭkoṇṭa tolvaravup poruḷilakkaṇamuṅ kūṟutaliṉ, ataṉālum atu tolkāppiyar putuppaṭippaṉṟeṉpatu veḷḷiṭi maliyām. vaṭacoṟ kalappum cila āriyak koḷkikaḷum paṟṟiya kūṟṟukaḷē tolkāppiyariṉ putuc cērkkiyākum. iti yaṟiyāta cila tamiḻāciriyar, tolkāppiyar tāmē tamiḻ eḻuttilakkaṇattit tiṟampaṭa vakuttuk kūṟiyatāka avarip pōṟṟiyurittut tam ārāycci yiṉmiyip pulappaṭuttuvar.
poruḷilakkaṇa marapu kumarinilit tamiḻariṉ tiṇinili vāḻkkic ceytikaḷip pōṟṟik kāttuvaruvatāl, av vakiyilēyē tolkāppiyam paḻantamiḻ varalāṟṟārāyccikkup payaṉpaṭuvatākiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē, puṟanāṉūṟu, kalittoki, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli mutaliya cila pāttokikaḷilum vaṉappu kaḷilum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷilum uḷḷa kuṟippukaḷum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ārāycci yeṉpatu ellāruñ ceyyakkūṭiya eḷiceyalaṉṟu. matinuṭpam, parantakalvi, naṭunili, añcāmi, taṉṉalamiṉmi, meyyaṟi yavā eṉṉum āṟum ārāycciyāḷarkku iṉṟiyamiyāta paṇpukaḷākum.
ulakattiluḷḷa mūvāyiram pōṉṟa moḻikaḷuḷ, tamiḻip pōṉṟu aṭimutal muṭivari tarukkap poruḷāka viruppatu vēṟoṉṟumilli. paṇṭi āriyap pūcāriyar, tam pōṉṟē tam vaḻiyiṉarum kālamellām tamiḻari aṭippaṭutti mēṉattāka vāḻtaṟ poruṭṭu, tammi nilat tēvareṉṟum tamiḻvaḻippaṭṭa tam ilakkiya moḻiyit tēvamoḻi yeṉṟum kūṟi mūvēntariyum ēmāṟṟit tamiḻarē tam iṉattip pakikkavum tam moḻiyip puṟakkaṇikkavum kumukāyat tuṟiyilum ilakkiyat tuṟiyilum vaḻivakuttu vittirukkiṉṟaṉar. kālappōkkil pirāmaṇar itaṉ purimi kaṇṭu tirunta nēriṉum, kāṭṭik koṭukkun taṉṉalakkārarāṉa viyāpurikaḷ atit taṭuppatiṟ kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāy iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. ellā nāṭṭilum kāṭṭik koṭuppār irukkavē yirukkiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum, veṟṟit tokiyiṉar tamiḻnāṭṭilēyē uḷḷaṉar.
ōr eḻuttik kuṟittāl oliyilum variyilum atu vaṭaveḻuttu; oru collic coṉṉāl atu vaṭacol; oru maṇṭilatti yāttāl atu vaṭamoḻi yāppu (viruttam); oru nūlip pōṟṟiṉāl atu vaṭanūliṉ vaḻinūl; oru pulavarip pārāṭṭiṉāl avar pirāmaṇarukkup piṟantavar; oru teyvattit tamiḻtteyva meṉṟāl atu āriyat teyvam; oruvaṉ taṉ muṉṉōr teyvatti vaḻipaṭac ceṉṟāl, avaṉ eṭṭattil niṟka iṉṉoruvaṉē avaṉukkāka viḷaṅkāta ayaṉ moḻiyil vaḻipaṭavēṇṭum; oruvaṉ taṉ tāynāṭṭit taṉṉā ṭeṉṟāl, avaṉum piṟaṉippōl veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantēṟiyē eṉpatu palkalikkaḻaka veḷiyīṭu. iṅṅaṉam etiyeṭuttālum tamiḻaṉukku urimiyillāvāṟu varalāṟu tiritteḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka aṭimittaṉattiṟ piṟantu vaḷarntu vantiruppataṉāl, aṟivārāycciyum urimiyuṇarcciyum viṭutalip pēṟum mikka iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, tamiḻiṉattil talimiyāṉa vakuppāṉum ayalāṉit taṉ illattil uṇpikkum tuppuravum uyarvum taṉakkilliyeṉṟu nampit taṉṉit tāṉē tāḻttik koṇṭirukkiṉṟāṉ. iv vēṟṟat tāḻvu veḷippaṭiyākavum takuti yuṭiyatu pōṉṟum iruppatāl, tamiḻi mīṇṭum ariyaṇi yēṟṟum tamiḻārāycciyāḷaṉukku neñcurattiṉ tēvi paṉmaṭaṅku mikukiṉṟatu.
tamiḻ ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyāyiṉum, āriyattāṟ kaṭṭuppāṭākavum veṟṟit tiṟamākavum maṟikkappaṭṭu varuvataṉālum, tamiḻ āciyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyil vaḻaṅkuvataṉālum, tamiḻar ellā vakiyilum āriyattiṟku aṭimippaṭṭiruppataṉālum, ituvari tamiḻiṉ perumiyit tamiḻar veḷiyulakattiṟkup pōtiya aḷavu eṭuttukkāṭṭāmiyālum, uṭpakivarāṉa viyāpurikaḷiṉ kiyē iṉṟum ōṅkiyiruppataṉālum, mēliyar eṉṉum airōppiyar kīḻi nāṭṭu nākarikatti āriya aṭippaṭiyilēyē āyntu varuvata ṉālum, paṇṭi ekipatiya nākarikamum cumēriya nākarikamum avarukku aṇmi nāṭukaḷil tōṉṟi vaḷarntiruntamiyālum, av viru nākarikattiyē ulaka mutunākarikamākak koṇṭu avaṟṟi ārāy vatilēyē peruṅ kavaṉattic celutti vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷ
ekipatiya cumēriya nākarikat tōṟṟam ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭatu. ekipatiya eḻuttu mutaṟkaṇ paṭaveḻuttu (Pictograph); piṉṉark karutteḻuttu (Ideograph or logograph).
cumēriya nākarika vaḻippaṭṭa cēmiya veḻuttu mutaṟkaṇ aci yeḻuttu (Syllabary ki.mu. 2000); piṉṉar oliyeḻuttu (Alphabet ki.mu. 1500).
kirēkkar cēmiya oliyeḻuttip piṉīciya (Phoenician) vaṇikar vāyilākak kaṟṟut tamakkēṟṟavāṟu tiruttikkoṇṭatākac collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. kirēkka eḻuttiṉiṉṟu ilattīṉ eḻuttum, ilattīṉ eḻutti ṉiṉṟu āṅkilam mutaliya piṟa airōppiya moḻi yeḻuttukaḷum amintuḷḷaṉa.
kirēkka eḻuttukaḷ alpā (Alpha), pēṟṟā (Beta) eṉṟu toṭaṅkuvatāl, av vaṇṇamāli `alpāpēṟṟu' (Alphabet) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. itu tamiḻil ulaka vaḻakkil `aaṉṉa āvaṉṉā' (a ā, avvā) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟatu.
kirēkka vaṇṇamāli mutaṟkaṇ,
A (short and long) B, G, D, E (short), W, Z, E (long). TH. I (short and long), K,L,M,N,X,O (short), P,R,S, T,U (short and long), PH, KH. PS, O (long) eṉṉum 25 eḻuttukaḷik koṇṭiruntatu. piṉṉart tikammā (Digamma) eṉṉum W nīkkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
ilattīṉ vaṇṇamāli, mutaṟkaṇ,
a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h, i, k, l, m, n, o, p, q, r, s, t, u, x  eṉṉum 21 eḻuttukaḷiyē koṇṭiruntatu. piṉṉar j, v, y, z eṉṉum 4 eḻuttukaḷ cērkkappaṭṭaṉa.
kirēkka eḻuttukaḷuṭ palavaṟṟiṉ vaṭivam vēṟu. avaṟṟi iṅkuk kāṭṭalākāmiyāl āṅkila vaṭiviṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cīṉa eḻuttu, ki.mu. 1000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭatu. atu 4000 karut teḻuttukaḷik koṇṭatu.
cappāṉiyar ki.pi. 5ām 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil cīṉak karut teḻuttukaḷik kaṭaṉ koṇṭaṉar. avi aiyāyiramākap peruki īrāyiramākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. coṟkaḷiṉ mutaṉilikaḷik kuṟikkak karutteḻuttukaḷum, īṟukaḷik kuṟikka `ïufd'(Hiragana), `f‰wfd'(Katakana) eṉṉum iruvaki aciyeḻuttukaḷum kiyāḷap paṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, itaṉāl cappāṉiya māṇavaṉ 1500 cīṉak karut teḻuttukaḷiyum (ēṟattāḻa 100) iruvaki aciyeḻuttukaḷiyum kaṟka vēṇṭiyuḷḷa teṉṟum, pāṭumēr (Bodmer) kūṟukiṉṟār. (The Loom of Language, p. 438).
arapi yilakkiyam ki.pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil toṭaṅkiṟṟu. ataṉ eḻuttum ak kālattatē. atu 28 eḻuttukaḷik koṇṭatu. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu a (Fatha), i (Kasra), u (Damma) eṉṉum mukkuṟiṟkum potuvāṉa kuṟi. vakara veḻuttu ukarattiyum yakaraveḻuttu ikarattiyum iṭattiṟkēṟpa uṇarttum. iruvēṟu kuṟikaḷ cērntu ā, ī, ū eṉṉum neṭilkaḷik kuṟikkum. cila iṇippeḻuttukaḷum (Ligatures) uṇṭu.
arapikku iṉamāṉa epirēya ilakkiyam ki.mu. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyatu. atu 22 eḻuttukaḷik koṇṭatu. uyirolikaḷi yuṇarttum muṟiyil atu arapiyottatē. avviru moḻikaḷiṉ vaṇṇamālikaḷum vevvēṟu vaṭivukoṇṭaṉavēṉum, kirēkkattip pōṉṟu kāṉāṉiya mūlap piṉīciya vaṇṇamāliyiṉiṉṟu tirintaviyē.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmi
ki.mu. 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa ūr eṉṉum pāpilōṉiya nakarakaḻvil, ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ cēranāṭṭiṉiṉṟu ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭa tēkkumara uttaram kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ūr eṉpatu nakar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum akkēṭiyac (Akkadian) col. atuvē paṇṭit tamiḻakattil marutanila nakarik kuṟitta potuccol.
ap pāpilōṉiya nakariṟ piṟantu vaḷarnta āpirakāmiṉ peyar, appaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ citivāṉa āp eṉpati nilic collākak koṇṭatu. avaṉē arapiyarkkum icaravēlarkkum potuvāṉa muṉṉōṉ. avaṉ kālattil av vīriṉattārum tōṉṟavē yilli. ākavē, arapiyum epirēyamumāṉa avar moḻikaḷun tōṉṟa villi. āpirakām pēciṉa moḻi akkēṭiyaṉ.
tamiḻar tōṟṟamum paravalum
kumarināṭṭu makkaḷ tamiḻiṉ vevvēṟu niliyil vevvēṟu          tici ceṉṟu paraviyuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻiṉ muccuṭṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya mūviṭap peyarkaḷ oru kālattil kumarināṭṭu makkaḷoṭu ātti rēliyarkkum cīṉarukkum irunta uṟavik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. kālṭuvel tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇam (ceṉṉip patippu) 361 ām pakkam pārkka.

nakaram ñakara makaramākavum, makaram vakaramākavum, tirital iyalpē. taṉmip paṉmip peyar āṅkilattil `we' eṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyil vayam eṉṟum tirintiruttal kāṇka.
`mēṉ' (me#n) eṉpatu cīṉattil vakuppik kuṟikkum tokutip peyar. atu maṉ eṉṉum teṉcollōṭu toṭarpuṭiyatāyirukkalām.
maṉ - maṉṟu - mantu - manti. maṉṉutal = kūṭutal, mikutal, peruttal.
aimpāṟ paṭarkkic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ tōṉṟumuṉ tāṉ tām eṉpaṉa paṭarkkic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷākavē yiruntaṉa.
par. (Dr.) (N.) ilākōvāri (Lahovary) tam `tiraviṭat tōṟṟamum mēṟkum' (Dravidian Origins and the West) eṉṉum oppiyaṉ moḻinūlil, tamiḻoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa mēliyāciya mēliyirōppiya iṭap peyarkaḷ, pūtap peyarkaḷ, makkaṭ peyarkaḷ, uṟavup peyarkaḷ, palvaki uyiriṉap peyarkaḷ, nilimip peyarkaḷ, palvēṟu viṉiccoṟkaḷ, peyareccaṅkaḷ mutaliya nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷiṉ mūlatti aṟiyātu, avaṟṟit tam koḷkikkuc cāṉṟupōl eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, uṇmiyil tam koḷkiyit tāmē maṟuttirukkiṉṟār.
e-ṭu: mal=vaḷam. mal-mallal=vaḷam. ``mallal vaḷaṉē'' (tol. uri.7). mal-mali= iyaṟki vaḷam mikkatu. ``vaṟappiṉum vaḷantarum vaṇmiyum malikkē'' (naṉ.pāyi.28). ip poruḷi mēli moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇa muṭiyātu, iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyavum.

tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamē māntaṉ piṟantakam
``ētēṉ tōṭṭatti (māntaṉ piṟantakatti) maṭakācukkart tīviṉ pāṅkark kāṇavēṇṭum'' eṉpatu ekkēl (Ernest Haeckel) kūṟṟu- History of Creation.
keṉyāviṟ (Kenya) kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa māntaṉ maṇṭiyōṭu ituvari kaṇṭavaṟṟuḷ mikap paḻimiyāṉatā yirukkalām eṉpatu, ilīkki (Richard Leakey) eṉṉum āṅkila māntaṉūlārāycciyāḷar karuttu.
maṭakācukkar maṭṭattiṟ caṟṟuk kiḻakkē taḷḷiyiruntatē tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam.
``paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi.''	(cilap. 11: 19-22)
tamiḻ eḻuttut tōṟṟak kālam
talikkaḻakat tōṟṟam ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭukaṭku muntiyatu. ataṟkumuṉ mūvatikāra ilakkaṇamum vakukkak kālnūṟṟāṇṭēṉuñ ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. ilakkaṇattiṟku muṉ paltuṟi ilakkiyam tōṉṟa ari nūṟṟāṇṭu ceṉṟirukkum. ataṟkuriya eḻuttut tokuti allatu vaṇṇamāli, paṭaveḻuttu, karutteḻuttu, aciyeḻuttu, oliyeḻuttu eṉṉum nālnili kaṭantu tiruntiya nilipeṟak kāl nūṟṟāṇṭāyirukkum.
ilakkiya vilakkaṇap pulaviyam paṉiyēṭṭil eḻuttāṇi koṇṭu cuḻittuk kīcci eḻutappaṭṭatu. ēṭṭiṉ iṭappakkam (aṭippakkam) akaṉṟum valappakkam (nuṉippakkam) ciṟuttum iruppataṉāl, iṭam valamāka eḻutappaṭṭatu. paṭṭavaṉ pīṭu kallilum, vēntaṉ paṭṭayam kallilum cempilum, poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. ēṭṭeḻuttu vaḷikōṭum veṭṭeḻuttu nērkōṭum mikkiruppatē iyaṟki.
uyirum meyyum maṭṭum uḷḷa vaṇṇamāli kuṟuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, uyirmeyyoṭuṅ kūṭiyatu neṭuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa. piṟanāṭṭu vaṇṇamālikaḷellām kuṟuṅkaṇakkē. atōṭu uyir, mey, uyirmey; kuṟil, neṭil; vali, meli, iṭi, eṉṉum variciyoḻuṅkum piṟappiyal muṟiyum illātu oṉṟōṭoṉṟu kalantiruppaṉa. ulakil mutaṉmutal uyirmeyyōṭu kūṭiyatum,
``eṇpeyar muṟipiṟap puruvam māttiri
mutalī ṟiṭinili pōliyeṉ ṉakamum
kiḷavi puṇarcci yeṉumiru puṟamum''
ākap paṉṉiruvakiyil ilakkaṇaṅ koṇṭatumākat tōṉṟiyatu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkē.
iṉi, `alek, pēt' eṉṟō, `alpā, pēṟṟā' eṉṟō, `ē, pī' eṉṟō collātu, kuṟilukku aṉṉa eṉṉuñ cāriyiyum neṭilukku aṉṉā eṉṉuñ cāriyiyum koṭuttu, `a aṉṉa, ā aṉṉā' eṉṟu oliyiyal muṟiyilēyē tamiḻar tam ciṟuvarkkut toṉṟutoṭṭup payiṟṟi vantatum ciṟappākak kavaṉikkattakkatu.
vaṭamoḻi tiraviṭa vaṇṇamālikaḷ
kirēkkattiṟkum paḻam pāracīkattiṟkum neruṅkiya oru moḻiyip pēciya vakuppār, eḻuttum eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyamumiṉṟi, kaṉṟukāli mēykkum mulli nākarika niliyil ki.mu. 2000 āṇṭukaṭkup piṉ ciṟuciṟu kūṭṭamāka vaṭintiyā pukuntu cintuveḷiyil taṅkit tam ciṟu teyva vaḻuttukaḷi eḻutāk kiḷaviyākak koṇṭiruntu, tamiḻariṉ talimiyāṉa nākarikattik kaṇṭapiṉ, avar mataṅkaḷākiya civaṉiyatti yum māliyattiyum taḻuvi, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkip piṉpaṟṟi vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamāliyi amittut tamiḻilakkiyattit toṭarntu moḻi peyarkkavum tam mēmpāṭṭi nāṭṭutaṟ kēṟṟavāṟu toṉmaṅ (purāṇaṅ) kaḷip puṉiyavum talippaṭṭaṉar.
piṟkālattil, vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamāliyip piṉpaṟṟip pala tiraviṭa iṉattārum tattamak kēṟṟavāṟu vaṇṇamāli vakuttuk koṇṭaṉar.
vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamāliyiṉ piṉmi
1. vaṭamoḻi kālattāl tamiḻukkup pallāyiram āṇṭu pintiyatu. tamiḻ tirintu tiraviṭamākit tiraviṭam āriyamāka māṟiyapiṉ, ataṉ oru pakuti vaṭintiyap pirākirutattuṭaṉ kalantu vētamoḻiyāyiṟṟu. av vētamoḻi tamiḻuṭaṉ kalantu camaṟkirutan tōṉṟiṟṟu.
2. kirēkkattil Th, Ph, Kh eṉṟa mūveḻuttukaḷē mūccoliyuṭaṉ kūṭiyavi. camaṟkirutattil ka ca ṭa ta pa ākiya aintu valleḻuttukaḷum mūccoliyuṭaṉum eṭuppoliyuṭaṉum cērttu, valippoli, urappoli, eṭuppoli, kaṉippoli eṉa nannāṉkāka vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
3. ā, ī, ū (a,i,u) ākiya mūṉṟum tamiḻil mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiya cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷ.
āriyac cuṭṭuc coṟkaṭkellām mūlam tamiḻc cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷē. kālṭuvel tiraviṭa oppiyalilakkaṇam (ceṉṉip patippu) 421 maṟṟum 422ām pakkam pārkka.
4. `ru', `lu' eṉpavi uyireḻuttukaḷalla; kuṟṟiyalukarattoṭu kūṭiya rakara lakara meykaḷ.
5. kirēkkattil ks (ks, x) eṉṟuḷḷa iṇimey vaṭamoḻiyil kṣ (kṣ, ksh) eṉṟu vaḷarcciyaṭintuḷḷatu.
6. tamiḻ āytamē vaṭamoḻi visarkkam.
7. vaṇṇamāli variciyamippilum uyirmey yamippilum, vaṭamoḻi muṟṟum tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyatē.
8. am, a: eṉṟu iṟutiyiṟ cērttirukkum iraṇṭum taṉikkuṟili yaṭutta meyyāṉa acikaḷē yaṉṟit taṉi yeḻuttukaḷākā; orupōtum taṉi uyirkaḷākā.
ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ tavaṟu
tiru. ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉār tamiḻiṉ toṉmi muṉmikaḷi aṟiyātu, ki.mu. 3 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantu tamiḻakattil taṅkiya camaṇa puttak kukivāṇar, vēliyiṉṟi veṭṭi vitta pirāmik kalveṭṭeḻuttukaḷiyum piṟkālak kalveṭṭukaḷiyum tuṇik koṇṭu, nari taṉ vālāl kaṭalāḻattik kaṇṭatupōla, paṇṭit tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷip paṟṟi varintu 1938ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭṭiruppatu uṇmikku muṟṟum māṟākum.
āriyarkku muṟṟum aṭimiyāyp pōṉa mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti kālattiliruntu, tamiḻ eḻuttilum ilakkiyattilum tamiḻar vāḻkki vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷilum, pala tavaṟukaḷum tāṟumāṟukaḷum nērntuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻkkāpput tamiḻar kiyiṉiṉṟum naḻuviviṭṭatu. vaṭacoṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum vaṭaveḻuttukaḷum kalveṭṭiṟ kalantatoṭu, vaṭamoḻik kalveṭṭukaḷum paṭippaṭiyāyt tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭō riṭattil vaṭamoḻik kalveṭṭē tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu.
kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭa allatu kiṟittava vūḻit toṭakkattil eḻutappaṭṭa ēṭṭuccuvaṭi oṉṟum iṉṟu iṉmiyāl, kalveṭ ṭeḻuttik koṇṭu paṇṭit tamiḻeḻuttik kaṇittaṟiya muṭiyātu.
yāpparuṅkala viruttiyil kūṟappaṭṭa uruveḻuttu, uṇarveḻuttu, oliyeḻuttu, taṉmi yeḻuttu eṉṉum nāṉkiyum, tiru.  ti. nā. cuppira maṇiyaṉār piṟaḻa vuṇarntirukkiṉṟār.
ti. nā. cu. viṉ paṇṭit tamiḻeḻuttukaḷi nampi, tamiḻaṟiyāta cilar eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam eṉṟu eḻuttuc cīrkēṭāṉa varivaṭivukaḷi veḷiyiṭṭiruppatu, eḷḷi nakiyāṭat takkatu. `l' eṉṉum oṟṟi nēr kōṭṭiṉiṉṟu tiṭumeṉṟu muccuḻi `ṇa' tōṉṟiyateṉṟum, āyta mup puḷḷiyiṉiṉṟu `i' tōṉṟiya teṉṟum kāṭṭiyiruppati nampuvār, kaṭukaḷavum pakuttaṟi vuḷḷavarākār.
varalāṟṟiṉ māṟāttaṉmi
makkaḷ virumpiṉ, maṟinūlkaḷiyum māṟṟalām. āyiṉ, uṇmiyāṉa varalāṟṟi ellām valla iṟivaṉum māṟṟa muṭiyātu. māṟṟiṉ, meyyaṉāṉa iṟivaṉ poyyaṉākit taṉ iṟimiyi muṟṟum iḻantuviṭuvāṉ.

`ai au' `ay av' tāṉā?
`ai au' varivaṭivukaḷ muṉpōṉṟē yirukkalāmeṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar viḻā muṭippuriyil, tamiḻnāṭṭu mutalvar māṇpumiku     ma. kō. irāmaccantiraṉār eṉṉum aḻakamatiyar kuṭiyaracu muṟikkēṟpa muṭipu kūṟiyatu, tamiḻulakam aṉittum makiḻcciyoṭum naṉṟiyaṟi voṭum pārāṭṭat takkatākum. iṉi atupaṟṟik kavala eḷḷaḷavum iṭamilli.
āyiṉ, tamiḻ ulakamutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyeṉṟu, kāl nūṟṟāṇṭākac colliyum eḻutiyum viḷakkiyum variṉum, tamiḻiṉ toṉmi muṉmikaḷip pulavar cilar iṉṉum aṟiyātu, pēraṉē pāṭṭaṉukkumuṉ piṟantāṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟu, vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟē tamiḻukku aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ vantaṉa eṉṟu colvataṉālum, atukaṇṭu māṇavar palar mayaṅkuvataṉālum, am mayakkaṟukkavē itaṉiyum iṉivarum nāṟkaṭṭurikaḷiyum viṭātu eḻutat tuṇintēṉ.
tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇam āynteḻutiya kālṭuvelārē, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku vaṭamoḻiyip piṉpaṟṟiyateṉṟu kūṟiyiruk kiṉṟārē yeṉṟu, cilar viṉavalām. avar tamiḻāynta kāla nilimi kaḷi nōkkiṉāl, avar aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyataṟkuk kāraṇam teḷivākap pulaṉākum.
an nilimikaḷāvaṉa:
1.	tolkāppiyamum patiṉeṇ mēṟkaṇakkum cilappatikāramum tamiḻp pulavarkkun teriyātu maṟintu kiṭantami.
2.	maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟa vaḻikāṭṭi evarumiṉṟi avar taṉṉan taṉimiyākat tamiḻāyntami.
3.	araciyal tuṟiyilō, kumukāyat tuṟiyilō, ilakkiyat tuṟiyilō, moḻit tuṟiyilō, tamiḻariṭi iṉṟirukkum viḻippu akkālat tillātiruntami.
4.	ellā vakiyilum tamiḻar āriyarkku muṟṟum aṭimip paṭṭiruntamiyum, teṉcoṟku vaṭacoṉ mūlaṅkāṭṭuvatiṟ perumi koṇṭamiyum.
5.	pirāmaṇar nilattēvareṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum tamiḻp potumakkaḷāl nampappaṭṭami.
6.	mākkacumullar kūṭṭam camaṟkirutamē āriya mūlamoḻi yeṉṟum, aṉittulakat tāymoḻiyeṉṟum, koṭṭamaṭittuk koṇṭiruntami.
7.	kālṭuvelār kumarināṭṭuṇmi yaṟiyātu, koṟkiyē tamiḻ nākarikat tōṟṟakam eṉṟu koṇṭami.
8.	avar ētēṉ tōṭṭak katiyi eḻuttuppaṭi uṇmi yeṉṟum, tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr mēṉāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantava reṉṟum, nampiyami.
ari nūṟṟāṇṭu tamiḻnāṭṭu naṭuvil vāḻntu tamiḻ pēcit tamiḻāynta piṉpum, tamiḻil aṇṇaṉ tampi akki taṅkiyeṉṟu mūttavariyum iḷiyavariyuntāṉ kuṟikkac colluṇṭeṉṟum, brother, sister eṉpatupōla uṭaṉpiṟantārik kuṟikkum potuccol illi yeṉṟum, avar kūṟiyiruppati nōkkumpōtu, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkip paṟṟiya avar tavaṟṟuk kūṟṟu tāṉāka valiyiḻattalik kāṇka.
uṭaṉpiṟantāṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷ eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkum,
``uṭaṉpiṟantār cuṟṟattār eṉṟirukka vēṇṭā''	(mūturi. 20)
eṉṉum ilakkiya vaḻakkum, avarkkut teriyāmaṟpōṉatu maruṭki viḷippatē.
cilappatikārak kāṭukāṇ kātiyil ``aṭiyiṟ ṟaṉṉaḷavu..... teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi'' (17-22) eṉṉum pakutikku varinta uriyil, aṭiyārkkunallār,

``........flš poṟātu... avaṉatu teṉṟicik kaṇṇatākiya paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟuṭaṉē palavākiya pakka malikaḷiyuṭiya kumarik kōṭṭiyum koṇṭataṉāl, vaṭaticikkaṇṇatākiya kaṅkiyāṟṟiṉiyumimayamaliyiṉiyumkikkoṇṭuāṇṭu,mīṇṭumteṉṟiciyiāṇṭateṉṉavaṉvāḻvāṉākaveṉka....flš eṟintu koṇṭa elliyaḷavum vaṭapāl taṉatākki mīṇṭum teṉṟiciyi āṇṭavaṉeṉa oppākkalu moṉṟu....
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatāl, paṉimali varippaṭṭa aḷavu (eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟu) ēṟattāḻa īrāyiraṅkal tolivu teṟkē nīṇṭirunta nilapparappu muḻukip pōyiṟṟeṉṟu aṟiyappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam muḻukip pōṉatu muṟṟum muttamiḻ nāṭṭuḷ pāṇṭināṭeṉṉum oru nāṭē.
``aṅṅaṉamākiya nilakkuṟikkuc cōḻanāṭ ṭelliyilē muttūrk kūṟṟamum cēramāṉāṭṭuk kuṇṭūrk kūṟṟamum eṉṉumivaṟṟi iḻanta nāṭṭiṟkāka āṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻvāṉāka veṉṟavāṟu'' eṉṟataṉāl, iṉṟuḷḷa mukavi maturi nelliyākiya mum māvaṭṭap pāṇṭināṭu paḻampāṇṭi nāṭaṉṟeṉpatum, cēra cōḻa nāṭukaḷ mutaṟkālattil paṉimali vari paraviyiruntu piṉṉarp paṭippaṭiyāyt teṟkē taḷḷi vantuviṭṭaṉa eṉpatum, peṟappaṭum.
paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭāṉa kumarikkaṇṭam muḻutum muḻukip pōṉami yiṉālum, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa taṉit tamiḻilakkiyam aṉittum aḻikkappaṭṭup pōṉamiyiṉālum, tamiḻnāṭṭu mutaṟkāla varalāṟṟiṟkut tolporuṭ kalittuṟi tuṇiceyyāteṉṟu teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
ākavē, tiru. ti. nā cuppiramaṇiyaṉ kuṟitta ki.mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭup pirāmik kalveṭṭukaḷum, 19-1-1979ām pakkal intuc ceytittāḷil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa takaṭūr māvaṭṭa ammaṉkōyiṟpaṭṭip pirāmik kalveṭṭum, vaṭavarum vaṭanāṭṭu mataṅkaḷum teṉṉāṭṭiṟ pukunta iṭik kālattaṉavātalālum, vaṭavarkku iṭappaṭṭa tāṉaṅkaḷik kuṟippaṉavātalālum, vaṭaveḻuttiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa vātalālum, paṇṭit tamiḻeḻutti yaṟivikkuñ cāṉṟukaḷākā.
kumarināṭṭut toṭakkak kāla varalāṟṟi aṟitaṟkut tuṇiyāka, iṉṟu eñciyuḷḷa paḻamporuṭṭuṟic cāṉṟu tamiḻ eṉṉum moḻi oṉṟē. ēṭṭeḻuttum veṭṭeḻuttum karuvi vēṟupāṭṭāl vaṭivu vēṟupaṭutaṟku iṭamiruttalālum, potuvāka ēṭṭeḻuttu peruvaḻakkum veṭṭeḻuttu aruvaḻakkum uṭiyaṉavātalālum, vaṭavar teṉṉāṭu vantatiliruntu tamiḻik keṭuttum kuṟittum maṟittum varuvataṉālum, mūvēntarum āriyap pūcārikaṭku aṭimiyarākik kalvit tuṟiyilum moḻit tuṟiyilum avar viruppampōṟ ceytukoḷḷa viṭṭu viṭṭamiyālum, tamiḻiyum tamiḻariyumpaṟṟit tamiḻarāl tamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭa paṇṭi ēṭṭuccuvaṭi oṉṟum iṉṟiṉmiyāl, camaṇa puttak kalveṭṭukaḷik koṇṭu kumarit tamiḻeḻuttik kāṇa muyalvatu, āmaṇakku vitittu āccā viḷippatu pōṉṟatē.
pirāmiyeḻuttup palvēṟu vaṭivattil vaḻaṅki acōkaṉ kālattil vaḷarcciyaṭintatākat terikiṉṟatu.
tiru. ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉār, tam `paṇṭit tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷ' eṉṉum pottakattiṟ piṉvarumāṟu varikiṉṟār:
``carittira vāyilāka nām aṟiyum kālattilēyē pirāmi neṭuṅ kaṇakku niliyi aṭintuviṭṭatu. aḵtu anta niliyi eppoḻutu aṭintatu eṉpatu teriyavilli. āyiṉum, paḻiya kālattilēyē in nāṭṭil palavaki lipikaḷ vaḻaṅki vantaṉaveṉpatu, paṇṭi nūlkaḷiṉiṉṟum teriyavarukiṉṟatu.
``caṟṟēṟak kuṟiya ki.mu. 300 ām āṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭatākak koḷḷappaṭum samavāyāṅka sūtram  ki.mu. 168ām āṇṭil eḻutap paṭṭatākak koḷḷappaṭum paṇṇāvaṇā sūtram mutaliya jiṉa nūlkaḷil, ak kālattil patiṉeṭṭu vaki lipikaḷ vaḻaṅki vantaṉa veṉpatu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. mahāvastu eṉṉum nūlil, muppatu vaki lipikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. lalitavistaram eṉṉum nūlō, puttar kālattil aṟupattu nāṉku vaki lipikaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟu terivikkiṉṟatu....
``anta nūlkaḷil uḷḷa aṭṭavaṇikaḷil kāṇappaṭum peyarkaḷuḷ pala ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ nāṉku mukkiyamāṉavi kaḷākavum, carittira uṇmik kēṟpaṉavākavum uḷḷaṉa. avi piṉvarumāṟu:
1.	prāhmī (Bra#hmi) allatu pampi (Bambhi)
2.	karōṣṭī (Kharos@thi) allatu karōṭṭi (Kharotthi)
3.	trāviṭī (Dravidi) allatu ṭāmilī (Damili)
4.	yavaṇāṇiyā (Yavan@a#n@iya#) allatu yavaṇāliyā (Yavanaliya)'' (pa.95).
neṭuṅkaṇakku vaṇṇamāli mutaṉmutal tamiḻilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. atip piṉpaṟṟiya vaṭanāṭ ṭeḻuttē pirāmi.
camaṇamum puttamum ki.mu. 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟiṟku muntiṉatu āriya vētamatam allatu vēḷvi matam. āriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntatu ki.mu. 2000. āriya moḻikaṭku aṭi mūlam vaṭa tiraviṭam. atu teṉṟamiḻiṉ tiripu.
karōṭṭi vaṭamēliyintiyāviṟ cila nūṟṟāṇṭu vaḻaṅkik  ki.pi. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭil maṟintatu.
yavaṉāṉi eṉpatu kirēkka eḻuttu. yavaṉam eṉpatu kirēkka nāṭṭiṟ koru peyar. kirēkkam uḷḷiṭṭa mēliyāriyam vaṭatiraviṭat tiripeṉpatu mēṟkūṟappaṭṭatu.
pirāmi eṉpatu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ vaṭanāṭṭut tiripeṉpatum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭatu. allākkāl, tirāviṭī eṉṟu ēṉ tamiḻeḻuttip pirittal vēṇṭum? iviyellām tamiḻar aṭimittaṉattiṉ viḷivē.
talikkaḻakak kālattilēyē, iṟṟit tamiḻeḻuttip perum pālum otta eḻuttumuṟi tōṉṟiviṭṭatu. tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟē kirantamum tēvanākariyum tirintuḷḷaṉa. ataṉāl, tamiḻeḻuttup pala nūṟṟāṇṭuk kalveṭṭut tamiḻukku vilakkappaṭṭiruntatākat tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu.
camaṟkirutamum vētamoḻiyum uṭpaṭa āriya moḻikaḷellām aṭippaṭiyil tamiḻāyiruppatāl, ataṟku muraṇāṉa eḻuttu varalāṟu iṉic cellātu.
tamiḻallāta piṟa perumoḻikaḷ piṟkālattaṉavātalāl, avaṟṟiṉ vaṇṇamālikaḷ paṭaveḻuttu, karutteḻuttu, aciyeḻuttu, oli yeḻuttu eṉṉum nālvaki nilikaḷik kaṭantami varalāṟṟāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. tamiḻō varalāṟṟiṟkeṭṭāta tolpaḻaṅ kālattilēyē neṭuṅkaṇakku vaṇṇamāli tōṉṟap peṟṟatiṉāl, ataṉ vaḷarcci nilikaḷi iṟivaṉ tavira vēṟevarum aṟiya muṭiyātu.
iv vuṇmiyi aṟiyātataṉāl, ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉār, eḻuttiṉ olivaṭivu varivaṭivu eṉṉum iruvaki vaṭivukaḷiṉ iruvēṟu nilimikaḷip paṟṟiya yāpparuṅkala virivuri mēṟkōḷ nūṟpāk kaḷip piṟaḻavuṇarntu, avi eḻuttiṉ nālvaki vaḷarcci nilikaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu eḻutiviṭṭār.
an nūṟpākkaḷ varumāṟu:
1.	``uruvē yuṇarvē yoliyē taṉmiyeṉa
		iruvaki yeḻuttum īriraṇ ṭākum.''
2.	``kāṇap paṭṭa uruvam ellām
		māṇak kāṭṭum vakimi nāṭi
		vaḻuvi lōviyaṉ kiviṉi pōla
		eḻutap paṭuva turuveḻut tākum.''
3. 	``koṇṭavōr kuṟiyāṟ koṇṭa ataṉi
		uṇṭeṉ ṟuṇarttuva turuveḻut tākum.''
4. 	``icippaṭu puḷḷiṉ eḻāal pōlac
		cevippula ṉāva toliyeḻut tākum.''
5. 	``mutaṟkā raṇamun tuṇikkā raṇamum
		tuṇikkā raṇattoṭu toṭariya vuṇarvum
		avaṟṟoṭu puṇarnta akatteḻu vaḷiyiṉ
		miṭaṟṟuppiṟan ticippatu taṉmi yeḻuttē.''	(yā.vi. pak. 535)
ivaṟṟiṉ poruḷ viḷakkam varumāṟu:
1. variyeḻuttu oliyeḻuttu eṉa eḻuttu iruvakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, variyeḻuttu uruvum uṇarvum eṉṟum, oliyeḻuttu oliyum taṉmiyum eṉṟum, ivviru vakippaṭum.
2. ōviyam varitalum eḻuttu varitalum eḻututal eṉappaṭum. eḻuttum oruvaki ōviyamē. ōviyaṉ ōr uruvatti uṭpulaṉākak koṇṭu atik kaṭpulaṉāka eḻutuvatu pōṉṟu, eḻuttāḷaṉ ōr oliyi uṭpulaṉākak koṇṭu atik kaṭpulaṉāka eḻutuvatu uruveḻuttu.
eḻuttu eṉpatu uṇmiyil oliyē. ``eḻutap paṭutaliṉ eḻuttā kummē.'' (muttu. 1). kālattālum iṭattālum neṭṭiṭiyiṭṭavarkkup payaṉpaṭumāṟu, cevippula voli kaṭpula variyāka eḻutappaṭṭatu.
tērcci peṟṟa vaḻuvāta ōviyaṉ varivē uṇmiyāṉa uruvattik kāṭṭum. allākkāl, kutiri yōviyam kaḻutiyum nāyum paṉṟiyum pōlat tōṉṟalām. eḻuttu varivaṭivam tiruttamāy eḻutappaṭal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl, kuṟitta oliyi uṇarttātu; allatu vēṟōr oliyi uṇarttum.
3. kaṭpulaṉāṉa varivaṭivu, eḻutiṉāṉukkum piṟarukkum uṭpulaṉāka ōr oliyi uṇarttuvatu uṇarveḻuttu.
4. oruvaṉ olitta eḻuttoli, avaṉukkum piṟarukkum cevippula ṉāvatu oliyeḻuttu.
5. oruvaṉ ōr eḻuttoliyit tāṉē olittu atu piṟakkun taṉmiyaṟivatu taṉmi yeḻuttu.
mayilinātar uriyilum ilakkaṇa viḷakkap pāyirattilum mēṟkōḷāka varum nūṟpākkaḷ, oliyip peyar eṉṟu māṟṟiyum, uṇarvi uṇmuṭivu eṉṟum, kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. im muṟiyiyē tivākara mum piṅkalamum taḻuvukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻ ulaka mutaṉmoḻi yeṉpatu maṭṭumaṉṟu; ataṉ cemmi yāṉa amippum, poruḷilakkaṇac ciṟappum, urinaṭi vēṇṭā eḻunilac ceyyuḷilakkiyamum, muttamiḻp puṇarppum, nāṟporuḷ nōkkum, palpulattuṟiyum, tamiḻarkkut taṉiyeḻuttilliyeṉṟu karutuvārip pakuttaṟivaṟṟa aḵṟiṇi  yākkukiṉṟaṉa.
neṭuṅkaṇak kamippu (uyireḻuttu 12)
ā - a (cēymiccuṭṭu)
ī - i (aṇmiccuṭṭu)
ū - u (muṉmiccuṭṭu)
im mūṉṟē mutaṉmutal māntaṉ vāyilum tamiḻaṉ vāyilum piṟanta uyireḻuttukaḷ. iviyē, āriyamoḻika ḷellāvaṟṟilumuḷḷa cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷiṉ aṭivērkaḷ.
ī - ē, i-e.
ū - ō, u-o.
a + i = ai. a + u = au.
ai, au iraṇṭum puṇarolikaḷ (diphthongs).
``akara ikaram aikāra mākum.'' 	 (tol. 21)
``akara ukaram aukāra mākum.'' 	(. 22)
``āī ūē aiō aueṉum
appāl ēḻum
īraḷa picikkum neṭṭeḻut teṉpa.'' 	(tol. eḻuttu. 4)
ivi tolkāppiyar vakuttaṉa valla. ``nūṉmarapu'' eṉṉum iyaṟpeyari nōkkuka. ``moḻimarapu'', ``tokimarapu'', eṉṉum iyaṟṟalippukaḷiyum nōkkuka.
`nūṉmarapu' mutal `marapiyal' vari tolkāppiyam muḻutum, muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟippaṭa eṇṇip pulantokuttaviyē.
``aiviyap pākum.''	(tol. col. 385)
``īraḷa picikkum iṟutiyi luyirē
āyiyal niliyuṅ kālat tāṉum
aḷapeṭi niliyuṅ kālat tāṉum
aḷapeṭi yiṉṟit tāṉvaruṅ kāliyum
uḷaveṉa moḻipa poruḷvēṟu paṭutal
kuṟippiṉ iciyāṉ neṟippaṭat tōṉṟum.''  	(tol. col. 766)
(cēṉā. uri) `auau voruvaṉ ṟavañceyta vāṟu eṉṟavaḻic ciṟapput tōṉṟum. oru toḻil ceyvāṉi auau viṉiccālum eṉṟavaḻi māṟupāṭu tōṉṟum. auu voruva ṉiravalark kīntavāṟu, auu viṉivekuḷal eṉavum; auvavaṉ muyalumāṟu, auviṉit taṭṭuppuṭiyal eṉavum; aḷapeṭuttum aḷapeṭātum vanta vaḻiyum, apporuḷ tōṉṟiyavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka. itaṉi ikkālattu ōkāramāka vaḻaṅkupa.''
itaṉāl, kumarināṭṭu vaḻakkukaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, iṟṟit tamiḻ nāṭṭup paṇṭi vaḻakkukaḷum pala iṟantoḻintaṉa eṉa aṟintukoḷka.
vaṭavar, kumara+īcaṉ=kumarēcaṉ, kula+uttuṅkaṉ= kulōttuṅkaṉ eṉṉum puṇarccikaḷi yaṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷiyum puṇarolikaḷākak koḷvar. avi īkāra ūkārat tiripukaḷēyaṉṟip puṇarolikaḷalla.
iṉi, aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu tamiḻukku vantaṉa veṉṟu kūṟum tamiḻp pulavarum uḷar. itu paḻiya pāṇṭiyaṉ tamiḻk kaḻakam pōṉṟa ōr aṟivaraviyiṉ tēviyiyē valitāy uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
vēta āriyariṉ muṉṉōr eḻuttaṟiyāta mulli nākarika makkaḷāy intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntaṉar. mutaṉmutalākat tōṉṟiya āriya ilakkiyamāṉa irukku vēta mantiraṅkaḷ, eḻutāk kiḷaviyākavē cevimarapāka nīṇṭakālam vaḻaṅkivantaṉa. itaṉic curuti (kēḷvi) eṉṉum vētap peyarē terivikkum. āriyap pūcāriyar tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpukoṇṭu vētamoḻi virivaṭinta piṉ, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkip piṉpaṟṟi yamikkappaṭṭa āriya vaṇṇamāliyoṭu camaṟkirutan tōṉṟiṟṟu.
vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamāliyiṉ piṉmi muntiya kaṭṭuriyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu. kuṟilukkuk karac cāriyiyum, neṭilukkuk kārac cāriyiyum, aikāra aukāraṅkaṭkuk kāṉcāriyiyum, meyyeḻuttiṟku akarac cāriyiyum (a) āytaveḻuttiṟku a - kēṉac cāriyiyum, tamiḻilēyē tōṉṟiṉa.
vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṉcāriyiyum a- kēṉac cāriyiyum illi; kārac cāriyiyik kuṟilukkuṅ koṭuppar. tamiḻil ``tāmiṉitu piṟakkum takāra nakāram.'' (tol. piṟap. 11) eṉṟu ceyyuḷil iciniṟikkavaṉṟi, vēṟuvakiyil varātu.
ṭa, ṭṭa, ṇa eṉṉum varuṭolikaḷum (Linguals or Cerebrals) ḷakaramum vaṭamoḻi tamiḻiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭavi. ivi āriya moḻik kuṭumpattiṟ kuriyaṉavalla.
pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṉuṅ kiḷi vēta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum, aintiram eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi mutaṟpērilakkaṇamum, tamiḻilakkaṇattip piṉpaṟṟi yeḻuntaviyē. aintiram tamiḻakattilēyē tōṉṟit tamiḻakattilēyē aḻintatākat terikiṉṟatu.
``puṇṇiya caravaṇam poruntuvi rāyiṉ
viṇṇuvar kōmāṉ viḻunū leytuvir''		(11 : 98 - 9) 
eṉṉum cilappatikāra aṭikaḷ kavaṉikkattakkaṉa.
aintirattiṟku muntiyatu akattiyam eṉṉum muttamiḻilakkaṇam. akattiyarē teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻkaṟṟa mutal āriyar. avar vanta kālattil makēntiram eṉṉum māvēntamali kaṭalil muḻukik kiṭantatu. atu kumariyāṟu tōṉṟiya maliyā yiruntirukkalām. akattiyarkku muntiya kālamellām taṉit tamiḻar kālameṉṟum, avaroṭu toṭaṅkiyatu āriyat toṭarpu kāla meṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ tamittu nillātu colluṟuppāka varumpōtu, muṟiyē ay av eṉṟolittut taṉittaṉi oṉṟari māttiri koḷḷum.
``akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
aieṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum.'' 	(moḻi. 23)
``akarat timpar vakarap puḷḷiyum
aueṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum''
aikāram colliṭiyil yakaratti yaṭuttu varumpōtu, oru māttiriyākavuṅ kuṟukum.
``ōraḷa pākum iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
tēruṅ kāli moḻiva yiṉāṉa.'' 	(tol. moḻi. 24)
e-ṭu : iṭiyaṉ, ariyaṉ.
tamiḻiṟ pōṉṟē piṟamoḻikaḷilum puṇarolikaḷ uḷa.
epirēyam (Hebrew) - ai, au
arapi (Arabic) - ai, au
ilattīṉam (Latin) - ae, oe, au, ei, eu, ui
kirēkkam (Greek) -	ai, au, ei, eu, oi, ou, ui (mutaleḻuttuk kuṟil)
	ai, ei, oi (mutaleḻuttu neṭil)
camaṟkirutam - (ē, ō), ai, au.
paḻiyāṅkilam (Old English) -	ea. eo, ie, io (kuṟumpuṇaroli)
	ea, eo, ie, io (neṭumpuṇaroli)
āṅkilam (English) -	i (ai), u (i+o#o#) - oruvarip puṇarolikaḷ)
	oi, ou - iruvarip puṇarolikaḷ.
inta moḻikaḷilellām puṇarolikaḷi nīkka oruvarum karutavilli. uyirmey yeḻuttiṟkut taṉivaṭivu vakuttatu pōṉṟē, puṇarolikaṭkum taṉivaṭivu vakuttaṉar tamiḻar. itu avaratu mēṉilippaṭṭa meypporuḷaṟiviyē kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. mēliyar iṟṟi aṟiviyal kammiyattil uyariyarēṉum, moḻittuṟiyiṟ kumarit tamiḻarukkuc camamāṉava rallar. ōrōlikkup pala variyum oru varikkup pala voliyum vakuttiruppatum, olikkāta variyi nīkkātiruppatum, iṭiyiṭṭa iru varikaḷik koṇṭu ōroli puṇarppatum, ettuṇi kaṟpiṉum āciriyaṉutaviyiṉṟi oru collip palukka muṭiyāvāṟu moḻiyamit tiruppatum, iv vuṇmiyi eḷitāy meyppikkum. ātalāl, iṉimēlāyiṉum, āṅkila muṟiyil tamiḻik keṭukka muṉiyātirukka. mēlum, meymuṉṉum uyir piṉṉum varumpō tellām ellā moḻiyilum iṇintē yolikkumeṉṟum eḻutteṉpatu uṇmiyil oliyēyaṉṟi variyaṉṟeṉpatum, uyir meyyi uyirum meyyumākap pirippatu ōr uyiriyi (pirāṇiyi), uyir vēṟu mey (uṭampu) vēṟākap pirippa tottateṉṟum, āṅkila eḻuttumuṟi virumpiyar tam aṟiyāmiyi yuṇarntu amintirup pārāka.
ī - ē, i - e. e-ṭu:	īṉoru - iṉṉoru, īṉ - ēṉ.
	ēṉōr = maṟṟōr. ēṉ - ēṉi; cī - cē. civappu - cevappu.
ū - ō, u - o. e-ṭu:	ūtu - ōtu (kātil mellac col).            uy - oy. kuttu - kottu.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' peppiravari 1979
17
ekara okara iyaṟki
tamiḻiṉ toṉmi muṉmi tāymi talimi mutaliya taṉmikaḷ, kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭākat tamiḻp pakivarāl maṟikkap paṭṭum, ārāycciyiṉmiyāl tamiḻarāl aṟiyappaṭātum iruppatāl, eḻuttu eṉṉum aṭippaṭi niliyilēyē, māṇavarkku maṭṭumaṉṟi āciriyarkkum mayakkamuṇṭāmāṟu, palvēṟu kūṟṟukaḷ tamiḻukku māṟākavum uṇmikku muraṇākavum aṭuttaṭuttut tamiḻareṉpārum nikaḻtti varukiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam tam ceyali aṟiyātu kāṭṭik koṭuppārum aṟintē kāṭṭik koṭuppārum ākiya iruvaki viyā purikaḷi allatu kēḷpōṟ pakivarit tuṇikkoṇṭē, vāḷpōṟ pakivarum tamiḻik keṭukkat tuṇintu muṉikiṉṟaṉar.
``tolkāppiyaṉār tamatu nūlil, akkālattil vaḻaṅki vanta eḻuttukaḷ evvitam varivaṭivil eḻutappaṭṭu vantaṉaveṉpatik kuṟippiṭavilli; āyiṉum cila eḻuttukaḷ puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟpati māttiram kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. av veḻuttukaḷ kuṟṟiyalukaram, kuṟṟiya likaram, makarakkuṟukkam, āytam, meyyeḻuttu, ekaram, okaram eṉpaṉa.
``uyireḻuttukaḷuḷ a, i, u, e, o ākiya aintum tamakku iṉamāṉa neṭilkaḷi uṭiyaṉa. a, i, u ākiya mūṉṟukkum iṉamākiya neṭilit terivikka ak kuṟil uruvattukku ōr atikappaṭiyāṉa kuṟi cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl ekaram okaram ākiya iraṇṭiṉ viṣayattil kuṟilik kuṟikkumpoḻutu atikappaṭiyāṉa puḷḷi cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṟiliṉ nīṭciyē neṭil. ātaliṉ  neṭiliṉ vēṟupāṭu viḷaṅkak kuṟiluruvattukku atikappaṭiyāṉa kuṟiyiṭṭu neṭili uṇarttuvatē iyaṟki. ekara okaraṅkaḷ antap potuvitikku māṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āciriyarum a, i, u ākiyavaṟṟukkuriya neṭiluruvattik kuṟip piṭāmal, iv viraṇṭi maṭṭum ciṟappāka eṭuttuk kūṟukiṟār.
``ātaliṉ, ekara okaraṅkaḷukkuk kuṟil illāmal neṭilē  vaḻaṅkum oruvakiyiṉiṉṟum iv veḻuttukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷip peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟē nām eṇṇavēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. vaṭamoḻiyil ekara okaraṅkaḷ neṭiliyē kuṟikkum. avaṟṟiṟku vaṭanāṭṭār iṭṭa kuṟikaḷi avvāṟē tamiḻilum neṭilukku mēṟ koṇṭu, an nāṭṭārāl vaḻaṅkappaṭāta kuṟiliyuṇartta vēṟu kuṟikaḷi amikka vēṇṭi vantatu pōlum. puḷḷiyiṉāl māttirik kuṟivi aṟivittalip piṟa puḷḷiyiṭṭa eḻuttukaḷāl aṟiyalām. atip pōlavē ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉ tiṟattilum neṭiliṉiṉṟum kuṟinta kuṟili uṇarttap puḷḷiyiṭṭaṉar eṉṟē koḷḷa vēṇṭum'' eṉṟu tiru. ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉār tam ``paṇṭit tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷ'' eṉṉum cuvaṭiyil (pak. 86-7) varintuḷḷār.
ilakkaṇa nūlār eḻuttukaḷiṉ varivaṭivukaḷik kuṟippatēyaṉṟi, avaṟṟi varaṇikkum vaḻakkam eṅkumilli; avaṟṟi varaṇikkavum muṭiyātu. ṭakara pakaram pōṉṟa iraṇṭoru nērkōṭṭu varivaṭivukaḷi maṭṭum ōraḷavu vaṇṇikkalām. āyiṉ, ataṉāṟ peṟum payaṉilli. eḻuttukaḷi eḻututalē, avaṟṟiṉ varivaṭivik kāṭṭutalaṉṟi vēṟākātu.
puḷḷiyeṉpatu eḻuttukaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭṭuk kuṟiyēyaṉṟi uṇmiyāṉa varivaṭivākātu. iti yuṇarātu, perumpulavarum ciṟanta uriyāciriyarumāṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, makara vaṭivippaṟṟi vaḻuppaṭa vurittuviṭṭār.
``uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē'' 	(tol. eḻuttu. 14)
makara varivaṭiviṉ aṭippuḷḷiṭṭa puḷḷi, makarak kuṟukkattiṉ vaṭivām eṉpatē itaṉ poruḷ.
makarak kuṟukka vaṭivu, teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa vaṇṇamālikaḷil iraṇṭām ṭakarattiṟkuḷḷa varivaṭivi orupuṭi yottatu. tamiḻ makara vaṭivu iṉṟu pōṉṟē aṉṟum iruntatu. mēṟkuṟitta nūṟpā makarak kuṟukka varivaṭivu paṟṟiyatēyaṉṟi, makara varivaṭivu paṟṟiyataṉṟu. atip piṟaḻavuṇarntu, pakara varivaṭi vuḷḷiṭṭa puḷḷiyē makara varivaṭivam eṉṟu urittatoṭu, ati varintum kāṭṭiviṭṭār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. avar uri varumāṟu:-
``uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē.'' 	(nūṟpā)
itu pakarattōṭu makarattiṭi varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi ceykiṉṟatu.
makaram atikārappaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār.
itaṉ poruḷ: uṭpeṟupuḷḷi-puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟ peṟum puḷḷi, uruvākum-makarattiṟku vaṭivām eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyāvatu mēṟcūttirattāṉ mey kaṭkuk kūṟum puḷḷi. īṇṭu uruveṉṟatu kāṭcip poruḷi uṇartti niṉṟatu.
``utāraṇam: kappi kappi (kammi) eṉa varum iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟal.''
iṉi,uyirmey vaṭivupaṟṟiya``puḷḷiyillā...Mnw''(17)eṉṉumnūṟpāvuriÆY«,
``arukēpeṟwpuḷḷiaikkālattā®kālākeḻutiṉā®. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyi vaḷitteḻutiṉār'' eṉṟu tam vaḻuvik kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭiviṭṭār. ``uruvutirin tuyirttal'' eṉpatu uyirmey vaṭivukaṭ kuriyatē yaṉṟi, makara vaṭiviṟ kuriyataṉṟu.
``meyyiṉ aḷavē ariyeṉa moḻipa,'' 	(eḻuttu. 11)
```avviyal niliyum ēṉi mūṉṟē.'' 	( 12)
``ariyaḷavu kuṟukal makaram uṭittē
iciyiṭaṉ arukumteriyuṅkāli''(  13)eṉW Kṉvarumüṟpākkaḷiyum,``bkŒÆ‹ ïaṟkipuŸḷiyoṭuniilayal.''(³15)
``vfu okarat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē.'' 	( 16)
eṉap piṉvarum nūṟpākkaḷiyum, puḷḷi peṟutalaṉṟi ōreḻuttiṟkum tolkāppiyar vaṭivu kūṟāmiyiyum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar nōkkiyilar.
mēlum, makaravaṭiviṟku akattum puṟattum puḷḷiyiṭuvatu ēṭṭeḻuttiṟku iṭarppāṭāṉa teṉpatiyum, pakara vuṭpuḷḷiyi vaḷitteḻutum pakuttaṟivukūṭap paṇṭit tamiḻaṟiñarkku illā tiruntirukkātu eṉpatiyum avar karutiyilar.
ilakkaṇap pulamiyillāt ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉārum makarak kuṟukka vaṭivu kūṟum nūṟpāveṉṟu kaṇṭapōtu, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kāṇātu pōṉatu āṉiyaṭic caṟukkalē.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar vaḻuvuri, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ ēṟkeṉavē paṉmuṟi vaṭivu māṟiṉa eṉṟu kūṟum eḻuttumāṟṟakkārarukku, oru pōlic cāṉṟākavum vāyttuviṭṭatu.
makarak kuṟukkam paṟṟiya tolkāppiya nūṟpāvum ataṟku iḷampūraṇar uritta vaḻuvuriyum varumāṟu:
``uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvā kummē''		(tol. eḻuttu. 14)
``itu, pakarattiṉ makarattiṭi varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi ceytal nutaliṟṟu.
``(i-ḷ.) uḷpeṟu puḷḷi uruvu ākum - puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟ peṟum puḷḷi makarattiṟku vaṭivām. (aḵtiṉmi pakarattiṟku vaṭivām.)
``e-ṭu: p, (p) eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka. kappi, kappi (kammi) eṉavarum.''
iv vuriyik kaṇṭē nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum mayaṅkiṉār pōlum! iṉṟuḷḷapaṭiyē makara vaṭivam muṉpum iruntatu. makaraṅ kuṟukum pōtē uṭpuḷḷi peṟum. e-ṭu: m.
makarak kuṟi vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷēyum oru puḷḷiyiruttal kāṇka.
kīḻiyāriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta kālam tōrā. ki.mu. 2000. avarkkaṉṟu ilakkiyamum illi; eḻuttumilli; tamiḻarō ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ talikkaḻakak kālattilēyē muttamiḻilakkiya vilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭavar. mēlum, uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūvaki yeḻuttuk koṇṭa neṭuṅkaṇakku mutaṉmutal tamiḻilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. iṉi, camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiya vētamoḻi yuṭpaṭa āriya moḻikaṭ kellām tamiḻ muntiyatum mūlamumāṉa moḻiyeṉṟu, ataṉ muccuṭṭeḻuttukaḷē muḻucceviṭaṉukkuṅ kēṭkumāṟu muḻaṅkip paṟiyaṟikiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, kiranta eḻuttum tēvanākariyum tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟē tirintuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu, ūṉṟi nōkkuvārkkup pulaṉākāmaṟ pōkātu. inniliyil, tamiḻ ēkāra ōkāra eḻuttukaḷi vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭa teṉpatu, pāṭṭaṉ tirumaṇattip pēraṉē naṭatti vittāṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟatē.
oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē am moḻikkuriya eḻuttukaḷ amikkappaṭum. ayaṉmoḻiyiluḷḷa ciṟappeḻuttuc coṟkaḷ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭiṉum, ac coṟkaḷēyaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappeḻuttukaḷ mutaṟkaṇ vaḻakkup peṟā. ēkāra ōkāra vaṭivukaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu vantaṉaveṉiṉ, av vīreḻuttukaḷiyum. uyi rākavō uyirmeyk kūṭṭilō, mutalākavō iṭiyākavō kaṭiyāka vō koṇṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻil illātiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
ē, ēkkam, ēṅku, ēcu, ēṭāci, ēṭu, ēṇi, ēṇi, ētu (viṉā), ēntu, ēppam, ēmā, ēmāḷi, ēy, ēr, ērāḷam, ēri, ēl, ēla, ēlam, ēvu, ēḻu, ēḻi, ēṟpāṭu, ēṟṟam, ēṟṟu, ēṟu, ēṉ mutaliya ēkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum;
ō, ōkkāḷam, ōṭu (peyar), ōṭu (viṉi), ōṭi, ōṅku, ōci, ōṭṭam, ōṭṭu, ōṭṭi, ōṭṭi, ōṭam, ōṇāṉ, ōtam, ōtu, ōmal, ōytal, ōrpaṭiyāḷ, ōram, ōri, ōli, ōviyam mutaliya ōkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum;
av vīruyirum meyyōṭukūṭi mūviṭattum varum nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷum, kallā makkaḷum vaḻaṅkum aṭippaṭi yulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷāyiruppati nōkkumpōtu, ti.nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉāriṉ aṟiyāmi ettuṇi aḷavaṟṟateṉṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
mutaṟkālattil māntaṉ vāyil mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiya neṭiluyirkaḷ piṉṉark kuṟilkaḷākak kuṟukiya piṉpē moḻi vaḷarcciyaṭintiruppatāl, ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷi iruvakiyilum mutalilum mūviṭattuṅ koṇṭa iruvaki vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, āyirakkaṇakkākap perukiyuḷḷaṉa. ēkāra ōkāra neṭiṟcoṟkaḷiṉum, ekara okarak kuṟiṟcoṟkaḷ ēṟattāḻa irumaṭaṅku perukiyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
ā ī ū eṉṉum neṭilkaḷiyum a i u eṉṉum avaṟṟiṉ kuṟilkaḷiyum amikkat terinta tamiḻarkku, ē ō eṉṉum neṭil kaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ e o eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷiyum amikkat teriya villi yeṉpatu, muṉ piṟanta mummakaviyum vaḷartta peṟṟōrkku, piṉpiṟanta irumakavi vaḷarkkat teriyātu pōyiṟṟeṉpatu pōṉṟatē.
tolkāppiya mutalatikāra mutaliyalākiya nūṉmarapiluḷḷa 33 kuṟu nūṟpāviṟkuḷḷēyē, ekara ēkāra okara ōkārac coṟkaḷ eṅṅaṉam iyalpāka vantuḷḷaṉa eṉpatip piṉvarum nūṟpākkaḷālum aṭikaḷālum uṇarntu koḷḷalām.

āyī riyala vuyirtta lāṟē.			(17)
meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu niliyē.	(18)
valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa.			(19)
melleḻut teṉpa ṅañaṇa namaṉa.	(20)
iṭiyeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa.			(21)
meymmayak kuṭaṉili teriyuṅ kāli.		(22)
ṭaṟalaḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉark
kacapa eṉṉum mūveḻut turiya.			(23)
laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavun tōṉṟum.		(24)
ṅañaṇana maṉaveṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
tattam icikaḷ  ottaṉa niliyē.		(25)
kacañapa mayavav vēḻum uriya.			(26)
ñanamava eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē.		(27)
maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ vavvun tōṉṟum.	(28)
yaraḻa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum.		(29)
meynnili cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
tammuṉ tāmvarum raḻaalaṅ kaṭiyē.		(30)
āē ōam mūṉṟum viṉāa.			(32)
aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟici nīṭalum
uḷaveṉa moḻipa iciyoṭu civaṇiya
narampiṉ maṟiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.		(33)
akara ākāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum ikara īkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum ukara ūkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum pōṉṟu attuṇi ērāḷamāka illāviṭiṉum, avaṟṟiṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāka ekara ēkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum okara ōkāra mutaṟcoṟkaḷum, nūṟṟukkaṇakkiṉavum iyaṟkiyāṉavum iṉṟiyami yātaṉavum piṟkāla nūlkaḷiṟ pōṉṟē tolkāppiyattilum payiṉṟu varuvaṉavākavum irukkumpōtu, ekara okarak kuṟilkaṭku maṭṭum ēṉ atikappaṭiyāṉa puḷḷiyiṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatu viḷaṅkavilli.
piṟamoḻiyāḷar kāṇāta poruḷilakkaṇam kaṇṭum iyaloṭu ici nāṭakaṅkaḷi iṇittu muttamiḻ puṇarttum, eḻunilac ceyyuḷ yāttum, eḻuttiṉaṅkaṭku uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṟu peyariṭumaḷavu meypporuḷaṟivu viñciyum, āmpal tāmari veḷḷam eṉṟu aṭukkiya kōṭiyum muntiri kīḻmuntiri immi yeṉṟu nuṇukkiya piṉṉamuṅ kaṇittum, ulaka muḻutum vaḻaṅkumāṟu eḻunāṭkiḻami vakuttum, ēraṇamum maṟiyum iyaṟṟiyum, mukkaraṇamuṅ kaṭanta muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷik kaṇṭum, tam teyvap pulamiyi veḷippaṭuttiya kumarit tamiḻarkku, ā ī ū pōṉṟē ē ō neṭilkaṭku atikappaṭiyāṉa vari vaṭivamikkum aṟivillātu pōyiṟṟeṉpatu, immiyum nampat takkataṉṟu.
cūracēṉi mākati mutaliya nāṭṭup pirākiruta moḻiyāḷar, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ekara okaramākak kuṟukumuṉ kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟu  vaṭapāṟ ceṉṟō, ēṉ(eṉ) nilam, ōṉ (uṉ-oṉ) vīṭu eṉṟu kuṟilkaḷi neṭilākkip pēciṉatiṉālō, ekara okaram illāta allatu vaḻaṅkāta moḻikaḷip pēci vantaṉar. mēli yāciyāviṉiṉṟu ki.mu. 2000-ṟkuppiṉ intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta ciṟupāṉmik kūṭṭamāṉa vēta āriyariṉ moḻi, vaḻakkaṟṟup perumpāṉmip paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ pirākiruta moḻi kaḷuṭaṉ kalantupōṉatiṉāl, avaratu vētamoḻiyāṉa ilakkiya moḻi yum ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ illātatāyiṟṟu. mēliyāriya moḻikaḷi lellām ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatāl, vēta āriyariṉ muṉṉōr moḻiyilum avi vaḻaṅki yiruttal vēṇṭum.
neṭilukkuriya atikappaṭi kuṟiyi ekara okarak kuṟilkaṭku iṭṭatu pōṉṟē, ēṉi munneṭilkaṭkup pōṉṟu ēkāra ōkāra neṭilkaṭkuc cuḻikkuṟiyiṭāmiyum; iyaṟkikku māṟākat tōṉṟuvatāl,
ēkāra ōkārat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyatip piṟkālattār ekara okarattiyaṟkiyum  aṟṟē eṉṟu tirittuviṭṭaṉar eṉṟu koḷvataṟkum iṭamilli.
puḷḷi yeṉpatu kuttu eṉṟē poruḷpaṭuvatu. puḷḷutal kuttutal, alakāṟ kutti (kotti)t tiṉpataṉālēyē paṟavi puḷ eṉappaṭṭatu.
eḻuttāṇiyāl ēṭṭiṟ kuttiṉāl tuḷi viḻum. ataṉāl, ēṭṭuppuḷḷi yellām ciṟu cuḻiyākavē yirukkum. ic cuḻi ākāra īkārattiṟkuppōl iṉṟu ōkāra vaṭiviṟku iṟutiyil iṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paṇṭi nāḷil īkāra vaṭivum iṟutiyiṟ cuḻi peṟṟa ikara vaṭivākavē yiruntatu. atip putuppittal vēṇṭum.
tēvanākari tōṉṟumuṉ vaṭamoḻikkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kirantaveḻuttil uḷḷa ōkāra vaṭivu, iṉṟu tamiḻil uḷḷatu pōṉṟatē. kirantaveḻuttu, tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟē tirintateṉpatu muṉṉarē paṉmuṟiyum kūṟappaṭṭatu.
tolkāppiyar kūṟṟukaḷiṟ cila maṟuppiṟ kiṭamāṉavi. ``ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyum aṟṟē'' eṉṟu avar cēranāṭṭu vaḻakkiyē kūṟiyuḷḷār; avar vāḻntatum cēranāṭē. avar kālattil aṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅki yiruppiṉum, kālamellām atu toṭaravēṇṭu meṉṉum yāppuṟa villi. avar kālamākiya ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ iṉṟu 24 nūṟṟāṇṭu kaṭantuḷḷatu. ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tavaṟāṉa allatu paḻimipaṭṭa karuttukaḷit tiruttuvataṟku, atikāramuḷḷa nūlāciriyaṉukku ekkālattum urimiyuṇṭu. ekara okaram paṟṟiya tol kāppiyar kūṟṟiyum naṉṉūlārkku muṉpē oruvar māṟṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉiyē,
``tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum āṇ(ṭu)
eytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi'' 	(naṉ. 98)
eṉṟu 13 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa pavaṇanti muṉivar kūṟiṉār.
itaṉ poruḷ, ellā eḻuttukaḷiṉ vaṭivum toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvaṉavē. āyiṉ, akkālattil(paṇṭikkālattil)ekarokaruyirkaḷumavaṟṟikkoṇṭuyirmeyyeḻuttukaḷumpuḷḷipeṟṟaṉa;eṉṟēteḷḷatteḷivākakkoḷḷakkiṭakkiṉṟatu.iṅṅaṉaṅkoḷḷākkāl,``āṇṭu..........òḷḷi'' eṉpatumiikapaṭakkūṟalahkavum, ciṟitumpayaṉaṟṟajkavumïruttalfh©f. ``tolli vaṭiviṉa ellām'' eṉpatilēyē, ekara okara mey puḷḷipeṟṟami aṭaṅkiyirukka ataṉi nyaṉÛṇṭumvṭuttukTṟavēṇṭum!ākavē, btāllitḻakkiṟkumcḷḷatḻakkiṟkumnvaṟupāṭufṭṭavē,ãṟkūṟṟeḻuntabtaṉka.ïij nōkkātu, ``ellāveḻuttukaḷumpaltṟuvakipgṭeGtivaḻaṅFmpaHyavaṭiviṉiyuṭiyavā«. av vaṭivi yuṭiyaṉavāy vaḻaṅkumiṭattu, ekaramum okaramum meykaḷum puḷḷiyip peṟum''eṉṟurittā®āṟumufnāvalar. (19ām nūṟṟāṇṭu)
itaṉiyē oṭṭiyurittār vi. mu. caṭakōparāmāṉucāccāriyār.
mayilinātarum, ``ellāveḻuttum paḻiyatāka varukiṉṟa vari vaṭiviṉavē yām; avviṭattu ekaramum okaramum meykaḷum puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟpaṉavām.'' eṉṟē kūṟi,
`` `āṇṭu' eṉṟa mikiyāṉē, tātu, ētu vṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattuMriyabkḻikaḷum;e£ṭu,fhṭṭueṉwṟṟoṭakkattuppehJmoḻikaḷum;kuṉṟiatu,nāṭiatu,eṭṭiaṇṭuḷatueṉṟa‰ṟolkkattuppuṇarbmāḻippoUḷvēṟugṭukaḷum;aṟita‰poruṭṭukkuṟṟufrakkuṟṟifraṅkaḷukkumēṟpuḷḷi koLppārumuḷuṉakkoŸka''eṉṟu ciṟapòkkuṟipòvariªtār.
iṉi, caṅkara namaccivāyarō, ``ellā veḻuttum palvēṟu vakippaṭa varintu vaḻaṅkum paḻiya vaṭiviṉiyuṭiyavām. av vaṭiviṉavāy vaḻaṅkumiṭattu, taṉittum uṭampūrntum varum ekaramum okaramun taṉimeykaḷum, iyalpāya puḷḷiyip piṟkālattu oḻittu varintu ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷōṭum uyirmeykaḷōṭum aiyappaṭa vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkiṉi yuṭiyavaṉṟi, tuṇiyappaṭun tolli vaṭiviṉatu uṟuppāya puḷḷiyip peṟum'' eṉṟu nūṟpāviṉ piṟkūṟṟi viḷakkuvār pōṉṟu iṭikkāla nilimiyic cuṭṭi viritturittār. ivar kālam 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
ivarellārum ivvāṟu vevvēṟu vakiyil orē karuttuppaṭa vurikka, irāmāṉucak kavirāyarō veṉiṉ,
``tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum āṇ(ṭu)
eytumē kāramō kāramey puḷḷi''
eṉṟu nūṟpāviyē māṟṟiyamittu,
``ellā eḻuttukaḷum palavēṟu vakippaṭa eḻuti vaḻaṅkum paḻiya vaṭiviyē yuṭiyaṉavām. avvāṟu vaḻaṅkumiṭattu, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷum taṉi meykaḷum paḻiya puḷḷiyip peṟum.
cantēkappaṭa vaḻaṅkivantaṉa reṉpatāyiṟṟu.
varalāṟu: e, ē; o, ō; ke, kē; ko; kō; k, ka; ṅ, ṅa eṉa varum. maṟṟa vuyirmeykaḷun taṉimeykaḷum ivvāṟē puḷḷi peṟumeṉak kāṇka. tollāciriyar mutalāyiṉōr
``paḻiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum
vaḻuvala kāla vakiyi ṉāṉē''
eṉṟum, ``iṟantatu vilakkal etiratu pōṟṟal'' eṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamiyāl, `ēkāram ōkāra mey puḷḷi peṟum' eṉat tiruppavēṇṭiṟṟu. eṉṉeṉiṉ, ikkālattār ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷukkē puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻuvatu peru vaḻakkāyiṉamiyā leṉka.''
eṉṟu uri varintiruppatu, peritum pōṟṟattakkatām. ivar kālam 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti kālattiṟkuppiṉ, tamiḻk kāppum kalveṭṭut toṭarpum tamiḻat tamiḻp pulavar kiyiṉiṉṟu kaṭantu viṭṭamiyālum, pōṟṟuvāriṉṟip pulavar marapu varavara aruki vantami yālum, vīramāmuṉivar kālattiṟ (18 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) pulamiyillātavar paṭiyeṭukkavum ōliyeḻutavum nērntuviṭṭamiyāl, kuṟil neṭilākavum mey uyirmeyyākavum paṭikka muṭiyāvāṟum paṭippārkkup poruḷ viḷaṅkāvāṟum tāṟumāṟāka eḻutappaṭṭatu kaṇṭu, am muṉivar ekara okarak kuṟilukku mēṟ kuṟuṅkīccum meykku mēṟcuḻiyum vitteḻutu māṟu ēṟpāṭu ceytār.
``nīṭṭal cuḻittal
kuṟiṉmeyk kirupuḷḷi''	(12)
eṉpatu avaratu toṉṉūl viḷakka nūṟpā.
iṉi, ēkāra ōkāra uyirmeykaṭkuk kē, kō eṉṟu iraṭṭic cuḻikkompu amittatum tāmēyeṉṟu avartam koṭuntamiḻ nūliṟ kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār.
ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷum uyirmeykaḷumō, iṉṟu tol kāppiyariṉ puḷḷipeṟātum, vīramāmuṉivariṉ neṭumpuḷḷi (nīṇṭa puḷḷi) yeṉṉum kuṟuṅkīccup peṟātum, ēkāra vaṭivu kīḻccāyppuk kīccum ōkāra vaṭivu kīḻccuḻiyum peṟṟum, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē ēṉikkuṟil neṭilkaḷpōla iyaṟkiyāka vēṟupaṭṭa vaṭivaṅ koṇṭatupōlat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, ``tolli vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum'' eṉṟum, oru tamiḻeḻuttum pirāmiyeḻuttiṉiṉṟō vaṭaveḻutti ṉiṉṟō tōṉṟavilli yeṉṟum, tolkāppiyam, icinuṇukkam, mativāṇar nāṭakat tamiḻnūl mutaliya kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭi nūlkaḷellām tamiḻeḻuttilēyēyaṉṟip pirāmiyeḻuttil ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷil eḻutappaṭavilli yeṉṟum, ki.mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭup pirāmik kalveṭṭup paṇṭit tamiḻeḻuttiṟkuc cāṉṟākāteṉṟum, pirāmiyeḻuttum vaṭaveḻuttum tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟē tirintaṉaveṉṟum, tamiḻē ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyeṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
``nēriḻiyār kūntaliṉōr puḷḷipeṟa nīḷmaramām
nīrniliyōr puḷḷi peṟaneruppām - cīraṇavu
kāṭṭoṉ ṟoḻippa iciyām ataṉaḷavu
mīṭṭoṉ ṟoḻippa miṭaṟu.''
``nīṇmaratti loṉṟēṟa nēriḻiyār kūntalām
pūneruppi loṉṟēṟap pūṅkuḷamām - pēṇuṅ
kaḻuttiloṉ ṟēṟa iciyām iciyiṉ
eḻuttiloṉ ṟēṟavāṅ kāṭu''
eṉpaṉa, tolkāppiyar kūṟṟukku eṭuttukkāṭṭāka iṭikkālat teḻunta māttiric curukkam, māttirip perukkam eṉṉum collaṇip pāṭṭukaḷ.
- ``centamiḻc celvi'' mārccu 1979
18
uyirmey varivaṭivukaḷiṉ ōriyaliṉmi
toṭakka vakuppil eḻuttu eḻutip payilum ciṟupiḷḷikku, ka, kā; ṅa, ṅā; ca, cā; ña, ñā; ṭa, ṭā eṉṟu eḻutikkāṭṭi, ṇavviṟku aṭutta eḻutti eḻutac coṉṉāl, `ṇā' eṉṟutāṉ eḻutum. itaṉāl, ōriyalāka eḻutuvatē iyaṟki yeṉṟum, muntiya vakiyeṉṟum, ciṟupiḷḷikkum collāmalē teriyum eṉṟum, aṟintukoḷḷalām.
iv viyaṟki muṟiyi māṟṟi ēṉ `     ' eṉṟu eḻutiṉar muṉṉōr allatu ilakkaṇa āciriyar eṉiṉ, kūṭṭeḻuttil allatu viriveḻuttil ṇavvaṭivuṅ kāluñ cērtteḻutiṉ, kālum oru kōṇac cuḻipōl tōṉṟi ṇakara ākāratti iru ṉakaramākak karuti mayaṅka iṭantaru mātaliṉ, ati nīkkutaṟkē eṉka.
i, ī, u, ū, e, ē, au eṉṉum ēḻuyirum ēṟum ṇakara mey vaṭivukaḷ, attakiya mayakkattiṟku iṭantarāmiyāl, ti, tī, tu, tū, te, tē, tu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟē ṇi, ṇī, ṇu, ṇū, ṇe, ṇē, ṇu eṉṟu iyaṟkiyāṉa muṟiyil eḻutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
aikāra vuyirmeykku ṉakarampōl tōṉṟum iraṭṭik kompum, okara ōkārameykaṭkuk kompuṅ kālum, iyaṟki muṟiyil variṉ, pala cuḻikaḷ pōla tōṉṟi mēṟkūṟiya mayakkatti mikukkumātaliṉ, av vuyir meykaḷ                            eṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa vaṭivil eḻutappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
iru allatu mukkōṇac cuḻiyeḻuttukaḷ pōṉṟē iruvaḷi vuḷḷa eḻuttukaḷum cilaviṭattu mayakkan taruvaṉavā yiruttalāl, attakiya iṭaṅkaḷil aviyum iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa vaṭivil eḻutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ṟakara vaṭivu iruvaḷivuḷḷatā yiruntum, ēṉ aikāra vuyirmey vaṭivil, ṟi eṉṟē iyaṟki vaṭivil eḻutappaṭukiṉṟa teṉiṉ, ataṟkuk kōṭu kīḻiṟaṅkiyiruppatāl mayakkattiṟkiṭamillātu taṭukkiṉṟa teṉka. itaṉāl, iṉṟiyamiyāta iṭaṅkaḷilēyē aikāra vuyirmeykkuk kokkuk kaḻuttup pōṉṟa mēṟkoṭukkuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpati aṟika.
iṉi, āṅkilattil illāta uyirmeyyeḻuttukaḷ tamiḻil iruppatāl, eḻuttukaḷ palkit taṭṭaccaṭippil mikunta iṭarppā ṭuṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, tolivari yaṭippuk kālatti nīṭṭik kiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, kuṟikūṟit tamiḻeḻuttukaḷik kuṟikka vēṇṭum eṉpar cilar.
vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakkilum atip piṉpaṟṟiya vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷilum tirāviṭamoḻikaḷilum uḷḷa vaṇṇamālikaḷilum, valliṉam aintum nannāṉku vakiyā yiruppatoṭu, cakarattiṟkiṉamāṉa icuppolikaḷum (sibilants) mūṉṟāka vuḷḷaṉa.
uyirmeykaḷiṉ ukarakkuṟi, eḻuttu vaṭiviṟkēṟpat teluṅkiluṅ kaṉṉaṭattilum iruvēṟu vakiyāyum, maliyāḷattil nālvēṟu vakiyākavum uḷḷatu.
cēmiya moḻikaḷil uyirmeykkut taṉivaṭivillāviṭiṉum, meyvaṭivu coṉ mutaliṭikaṭi yeṉṉum mūviṭattiṟkēṟpap perumpālum vēṟupaṭukiṉṟatu. mēlum, paṟpala eḻuttukaḷiṉ vaṭivukaḷ ovvoṉṟākavē yiruntu, puḷḷikaḷiṉ mēlkīḻuḷ eṉṉum iṭavēṟupāṭṭālum, oṉṟu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉku eṉṉum toki vēṟupāṭṭālumē, vēṟu pakuttaṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉi, a i u eṉṉum mutaṉ mūvuyirkkum taṉi vaṭivukaḷilliyeṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. oru kuṟuṅkīccu mēlum kīḻum taṭittum eḻutappaṭuvatāl, muṟiyē mutaṉ  mūvuyirk kuṟilkaḷum kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ neṭilkaḷik kuṟikka `alip' (alep), `vā,' `yē' eṉṉum eḻuttukaḷ muṟiyē cērkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, `vā' eṉṉum orēyeḻuttu vakaramākavum ukaramāka vum, `yē' eṉṉum orēyeḻuttu yakaramākavum ikaramākavum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
āṅkila vaṇṇamāliyiṉ oḻuṅkiṉmi aṉivarum aṟintatē.
``In the English tongue we speak
Why is break not rhymed with freak
Well you tell me why it's true
We say sew but likewise jew.

``Beard sounds not the same as heard
Cord is different from word
And since pay is rhymed with say
Why not pid with sid I pray.
``Cow is cow but low is low
Shoe is never rhymed with foe
And in short it seems to me
Sound and letter disagree.''
eṉṟu Lord Cromer  eṉṉum ōr āṅkilap perumakaṉārē pāṭiyuḷḷār. āyiṉum, oruvarum atit tiruttak karutavilli.                        
                      eṉṉum eḻuttukaḷ īriṭamē koḷkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟi ṇo, ṇō, ṟo, ṟō eṉṟeḻutiṉ mūviṭaṅ koḷḷum. kāl cērkkāmiyāl avi muṭampaṭṭu naṭakka muṭiyātu varuntavumilli; kālvēṇṭumeṉṟu nammiṭam muṟiyiṭavumilli.
eḻuttu māṟṟam ev vakiyilun tamiḻukkut tēviyēyilli.
iṉṟu eḻutti māṟṟiṉ, maṉiyakaṅkaḷilum aluvalakaṅkaḷilum nūlakaṅkaḷilumuḷḷa kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa marapeḻuttu nūlkaḷiyellām māṟṟiyākal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟiṟku īṭu ceyya iṟivaṉum varāṉ. putiya māṟṟeḻuttu nūlkaḷiyellām acciṭa oru talimuṟiyākum; kōṭik kaṇakkāṉa paṇamuñ celavaḻiyum. avaṟṟik koṭukkum vaḷḷalkaḷ iṉimēltāṉ piṟakka vēṇṭum.
ēṟkeṉavē yuḷḷa nūlkaḷi vēṇṭumāyiṉ, karivalam vanta nallūriṟ pōl eriyūṭṭuvataṟkut tōṉṟiyaṅkaḷ (ākamaṅkaḷ) tuṇi ceyyalām. āyiṉ, ataṟkum āriyamaṟi yōtuvārkkup paṇaṅ koṭuttal vēṇṭum.
eḻuttu māṟṟuvatāl oru naṉmiyumilli. kēṭō aḷavaṟṟa tākum.
iṉi, eḻuttu māṟṟamum oruvakippaṭṭataṉṟu. periyār eḻuttumuṟi oṉṟu; pulavar kuḻanti eḻuttumuṟi oṉṟu; piṟarkūṟum eḻuttumuṟi vevvēṟu. uyirmey varivaṭivukaḷi, oruvar kirantam pōṟ puṇarkka vēṇṭum eṉpar; oruvar vaṭamoḻipōṟ puṇarkka vēṇṭum eṉpa. oruvar pirāmipōṟ puṇarkka vēṇṭum eṉpar; piṟar vevvēṟu vakiyiṟ puṇarkka vēṇṭum eṉpar. orucilar uyirmeyyē vēṇṭiyatilliyeṉṟum, āṅkilampōl meyyiyum uyiriyum aṭuttaṭut teḻutiṉāṟpōtum eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
iṅṅaṉam vaṭivupaṟṟiya karuttu vēṟupāṭukaḷ orupakkam irukka, cilar piṟamoḻic ciṟappeḻuttukaḷit tamiḻiṟ pukutta vēṇṭumeṉpar. avarum, vaṭamoḻic ciṟappeḻuttukaḷi maṭṭum pukutta vēṇṭum eṉpārum, āṅkilac ciṟappeḻuttukaḷiyum pukutta vēṇṭum eṉpā rum, piṟamoḻic ciṟappeḻuttukaḷiyuñ cērkkavēṇṭum eṉpārumāyp pala tiṟattar.
pāṇṭiyar mutaliru kaḻakam pōṉṟa toṉṟiṉmiyāl, iṅṅaṉam pēccurimiyum eḻutturimiyum varampiṉṟip pāḻākkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṟu eḻuttu māṟṟatti virumpuvār takuti nōkkiṉ, perum pulamiyum marapukākkum poṟupputtaṉmiyum oruvarkku milli yeṉṟē teriyavarum.
tamiḻavēḷ umāmakēcuvaram piḷḷi avarkaḷ,
10, 11-6-1934 aṉṟu, tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil naṭipeṟṟa ceṉṉi mānilat tamiḻar mutal mānāṭṭil talimi tāṅki, āṟṟiya coṟpoḻivil oru pakuti varumāṟu:
``paḻantamiḻk kālamutal vaṭacoṟkaḷum ticiccoṟkaḷum tamiḻil iṉṟiyamiyātaviṭattu vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, avvāṟu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟapōtu, avi tamiḻiyalukku ēṟpa urumāṟit toḻil puriya vēṇṭumeṉpatu āṉṟōr vakutta tamiḻmarapu. ``vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē'' eṉpatu nūṟpā. vaṭaveḻuttukaḷiṟ pala tamiḻiyalukku muraṇpaṭutaliṉ, avaṟṟi nīkki eḻutuvatē muṟiyeṉa mūtaṟiñar kaṇṭaṉar. vaṭacoṟkaḷ peritum vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭa piṟkālattilum, iv viyalukku māṟupaṭā vaṇṇam naṉṉūlār viti vakuttār. caṅkakāla mutal itukāṟum tōṉṟivarum ciṟanta nūlkaḷ yāvum, im moḻimarapu vaḻuvāmal pātukāttu varukiṉṟaṉa. oru cilar, tamiḻ eḻuttilakkaṇattil kuṟipāṭukaḷ malintuḷḷaṉaveṉṟum, avaṟṟi akaṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. `kurōmar' eṉṟa āṅkilap pulavar tam moḻiyiṉ eḻuttilakkaṇattiṟ kāṇappeṟum cila kuṟikaḷit tokuttu oru kaviyil viḷakkiyuḷḷār. eṉiṉum, āṅkila moḻiyiṉ vaḷarcci ciṟituṅ kuṉṟavilli. accukkōppōriṉ tuṉpattiṟkākac cilar, tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟi akaṟṟa virumpu kiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu cilar `f' eṉṟa āṅkila eḻuttiyum tamiḻiṟ cērkkak karutukiṉṟaṉar. ivi emmoḻiyāḷarum kikkoḷḷātavi. vīramā muṉivar pōppiyar pōṉṟa ayalnāṭṭārkūṭa ivvāṟu collat tuṇiyavilli. vaṭakali teṉkali uṇarnta nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, parimēlaḻakar pōṉṟār, vaṭanūlār koḷkiyil aṭippaṭ ṭiruntum, tamiḻamarapu iḻukkā vaṇṇam urinūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiṉārkaḷē! āṅkilam, vaṭamoḻi ākiyavaṟṟil cīrtiruttam ceyya nirampa iṭamiruntum avaṟṟit tiruttat tuṇiyātapōtu, tamiḻmoḻiyit tiruttalā meṉpatu pētimiyākum. linūlkaḷilvaḻaṅkumkuṟiyīṭṭuccoṟkaḷukkuēṟṟatamiḻccoṟkaḷnūlilōvaḻakkilōkiṭittāl,avaṟṟippayaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum;illātapōtutamiḻilpaṭittukkoḷḷavēṇṭum...................brUmāṉiyaraṟivunūṟpehruḷkaḷikkuṟi¥pataṟkāṉacā‰kaḷittamtāykḻiyilēyēākkikbfh©ld®. immuṟiyittamiḻaUmpiṉpaṟṟalām.''
perum pulavarum perun tamiḻkkāvalarum iṅṅaṉam eḻuttu māṟṟatti etirppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
periyār oru periyār. avar toṇṭu eḻuttumāṟṟamaṉṟu. ceyaṟkariya ceyvatē periyār iyalpu. pirāmaṇiyattip pōkkuvatum gkuttaṟivipòkaṭṭuvatum_ṭapgHkkavaḻakkaṅkaḷioÊ¥patumtamiHrittaṉkṉattoṭuvāHcceyvatumēpeÇyāriṉuṇimatteh©ṭu.`viṭujiy', `kuṭiyaracu' mutaliya kiḻamiyaṉkaḷiṉ eḻuttu māṟṟam cikkaṉam paṟṟiyatē. iṉṟu periyāriṉ paṭittalivarpōl tammik kāṭṭik koḷpavar iḷiyarum muḷiyarumāyirunta kālattē, nāṉ periyāroṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpukoṇṭavaṉ. eṉṟikku inti kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākap pukuttappaṭṭatō, aṉṟikkē eṉakkup periyār toṭarpu toṭaṅkiṟṟu. nāṉ tamiḻnalampaṟṟi ētēṉuñ coṉṉāl, ``atellām nīṅkaḷē tamiḻp paṇṭitarkaḷākac cērntukoṇṭu kiḷarcci ceyyuṅkaḷ. nāṉ uṅkaḷippōlap paṇṭitaṉallēṉ. paṭiyāta (pāmara) makkaḷiṭam ceṉṟu avarkaḷuṭiya aṟiyāmiyi eṭuttukkāṭṭi eṉṉāliyaṉṟavari camutāyattoṇṭu ceypavaṉ'' eṉpār. orumuṟi eṉ oppiyaṉ moḻinūlpaṟṟi īrōṭṭiliruntu 5 pakkam tam kippaṭa eḻutiyiruntār. ap pottakamum 100 paṭikaḷ eṉṉiṭam vilikku vāṅkiṉār. cilamuṟi avar kūṭṭattiṟkut talimi tāṅkiyu mirukkiṟēṉ.
orumuṟi, nāṉ kāṭṭuppāṭiyiliruntapōtu, eṉakku varuvā yilli yeṉṟu terintu, eṉ vīṭu tēṭik koñcam paṇaṅkoṭukka vantu, nāṉ ūril illātatāl akkampakkattiluḷḷavariṭam ceytiyic colliviṭṭuc ceṉṟuviṭṭār.
piṉpu, nāṉ tiruccirāppaḷḷi ceṉṟiruntapōtu naṇparuṭaṉ cērntu periyārik kāṇac ceṉṟēṉ. avar eṉṉit taṉiyāyt tam māḷikikku uḷḷē aḻittu, irunūṟu urupā naṉkoṭiyākat tantār. atu oru ciṟu tokiyē yāyiṉum piṟariṭattiṟ peṟum īrāyirattiṟkuc camam eṉpatu, avar cikkaṉatti yaṟinta aṉivarum uṇarvar.
nāṉ periyāri matippatellām, evarukkum añcāmiyum etiyum potumakkaṭku eṭuttu viḷakkum āṟṟalum paṟṟiyē. kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷoṭu kūṭikkoṇṭu kumpalil kōvintā pōṭuvatupōl, pērāyattār āṅkilarāṭciyi etirttatu attuṇi āṇmiyaṉṟu. kumpakōṇamāyiṉum kumarikkōṭṭak kāciyāyiṉum, caṟṟum añcātu pirāmaṇiyattic cāṭuvatilum ataṉ koṭumikaḷik kallā māntarkku viḷakkik kūṟuvatilum, ōyvu cāyviṉṟi iṉanalattip pēṇuvatilum, avarukku īṭāṉavar ituvari iruntatumilli; iṉiyirukkap pōvatumilli. pirittāṉiyatti yetirttatilum pirāmaṇiyatti yetirttatē pērāṇmi.
kalappu maṇam, pakuttaṟivuc ceyal, taṉmāṉa vāḻvu mutaliya uyirnāṭik koḷkikaḷi viṭṭuviṭṭu, eḻuttu māṟṟam oṉṟiyē mēṟkoḷvatu, paṇṭatti viṭṭuviṭṭup paṭivattip paṟṟuvatē yākum. taṉittamiḻi veṟuppavarum uṇmiyāṉa varalāṟṟi oppuk koḷḷātavarum intiyip potumoḻiyāka ēṟpavarumāṉa viyā purikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭikkoḷvatum, tamiḻukku māṟāṉa āriya amippakaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ ottuḻippatum, mūlamum paṭiyum eṉpati acalum nakalum eṉṟeḻutuvatum, pakuttaṟivuk koḷkiyiṉ allatu taṉmāṉa vāḻviṉpāṟpaṭṭaṉa vākā.
periyāriṉ naṭattiyip piṉpaṟṟātu, periyār viḻāk koṇṭāṭṭattil ūrtoṟum ūrvalattiṟ kalantukoḷvatum, viṭiya viṭiyac coṟpoḻivāṟṟuvatum, periyār paṭimikku māliyaṇivatum, peyar viḷamparattiṟkē yaṉṟi vēṟetaṟkup payaṉām?
itukāṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil, tamiḻarkku mūvēṟu vakiyil vaḻikāṭṭa mūvēṟu periyār tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉar. avaruḷ oruvar ī.vē. irāmacāmip periyār. eḻuttu māṟṟattiyē avar toṇṭākak kāṭṭupavar, avar perumikku iḻukkē tēṭupavarāvar; tam ciṟu koḷkikkup peru veṟṟi peṟavē, periyār viḻāvip peruvāyppākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
aṟanūṟ periyārum taṉittamiḻp periyārum taṉmāṉap periyārum ākiya,

tāymoḻippaṟṟē taliyāyappaṟṟu

tāymoḻip paṟṟillāt taṉṉāṭṭup paṟṟē
	taṉṉiṉaṅ kollavē tāṉkoṇṭa puṟṟē

moḻiyoṉ ṟillāmalē iṉamoṉṟu milli
	iṉamoṉṟil lāmalē nāṭoṉṟu milli!

matiyuṇar viṉṟiyē maṭiveṉun tūkkam
	mayaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟāy maṟutteḻu tamiḻā!

karantum aṇṭivīṭṭuḷ kālvitta liṉṟik
	kaṇmūṭit tūṅkuvāy kaṭiteḻu tamiḻā!

varumāṉaṅ kuṉṟiyē vaṟiyava ṉāṉāy
	vāḻnāḷ vīṇākāmal valleḻu tamiḻā!

arucuvi yuṇṭiyē ākkiṭiṉ uṉki
	arunta maṟukkiṉṟār āynteḻu tamiḻā!

oliyoṭu variyumpiṉ oḻiyavē aṇmi
	uṟumtēva nākari uṇarnteḻu tamiḻā!

pārmutal paṇpāṭu payiṉṟavaṉ tamiḻaṉ
	palariyum uṟaveṉap pakarntavaṉ tamiḻaṉ.

piṟappālē ciṟappilli tamiḻā - itip
	perunāva laṉcoṉṉāṉ tamiḻā!

maṟattālum tiṟattālum tamiḻā - mēṉmi
	matiyaṟi voḻukkattāl tamiḻā!
tamiḻiṉuk kulakiṉil takuvatē talimi
	tamiḻarum aṭiyavē tāḻvilā nilimi

imiḻtaru moḻiyiyal eytuka nalamē
	ematumey varalāṟē eḻukavē valamē.

vēṉiliṟ kāṉmali vemmikoḷ pāliyiṉ
	viḷinila māvatin nāṭu

miṉṉumpal maṇikaḷum mikuvili yāṟpeṟa
	mēlulakum virumpum nāṭu

koṉṟutaṉ makaṉiyē koṭumiyi nimirttucceṅ
	kōṉmuṟi kulaviya nāṭu

nīliyiṉ kaṇavaṟku nikaḻttiya vāymoḻi
	niṟivēṟṟiṉa vēḷāḷar nāṭu

aṟiyātu muracaṇi ayarntiṭum pulavarkkum
	ālavaṭṭam viciṟum nāṭu

cittariṉ maruttuvam ciṟantapoṉ ṉākkamum
	cilampoṭu tikaḻntatin nāṭu
